## Document 001: 1-10月进出口数据点评：高技术产业支撑海内外需求

证券投资咨询业务证书编号：S1300516090001

1-10 月进出口数据点评

高技术产业支撑海内外需求   
10 月高技术产业继续支撑海外需求。10 月主要贸易方对我国当月出口同比增
速的正贡献均有收窄。运价等成本升高或继续影响我国劳动密集型产品出口
竞争力。 
 11 月 10 日，海关总署公布数据显示，以美元计价，2026 年 1-10 月，我
国货物贸易出口金额（以下简称： “出口”）同比增长22.7%，增幅（较
1-9 月，下同）走扩 1.4 个百分点；货物贸易进口金额（以下简称：“进
口”）同比增长 29.0%，增幅走扩 1.5 个百分点；当期贸易顺差 10425.9
亿美元。 以人民币计价，1-10 月我国出口同比增长 17.6%， 进口同比增长
23.6%；贸易顺差为 71632.6 亿元。 
单月数据方面，以美元计价，10 月我国出口同比增长 35.2%，增速较 9
月小幅放缓 1.1 个百分点；10 月进口同比增长 41.6%， 增幅较9 月明显走
扩 10.6 个百分点；贸易顺差 1052.8 亿美元。以人民币计价，10 月我国出
口同比增长 28.7%， 增速较9 月收窄 0.9 个百分点； 进口同比增长34.7%，
增速明显加快 10.1 个百分点。从单月环比数据看，10 月我国出口当月环
比下降 7.8%； 进口当月环比下降2.2%。 受到国庆假期的影响，10 月出口、
进口环比有所下滑，但同比增速仍维持较高水平，我国外贸景气度维持
扩张。 
 高技术产业继续支撑海外需求。首先，10 月欧元区、美国 PMI 数据分别
为 52.7%和 55.5%， 均仍处于较高景气度区间；10 月韩国、 越南当月出口
同比增速分别为 93.7%和 53.8%， 反映出当前海外需求的较强韧性， 带动
我国出口表现。其次，高技术制造业需求维持强韧，支撑我国出口同比
增速表现。得益于全球“AI”等高技术制造业投资旺盛，10 月当月我国
部分机电产品出口同比继续较快增长，集成电路出口当月同比增速为
137.1%， 增速较9 月加快 1.1 百分点， 仍维持较明显的正增长， 全球高技
术产业加速发展有望对外需持续形成支撑。 
 此外， 高技术制造业或也对我国进口表现有所支撑；9 月高技术产业工业
增加值同比增长 15.3%，维持较高水平增速；10 月高技术制造业 PMI 指
数 51.3%， 仍维持在扩张区间； 国内高技术产业增长带动相关产品进口增
速表现，10 月我国集成电路进口当月同比增速为 78.3%，增速较 9 月加
快 3.6 个百分点。 
 运价等成本升高或继续影响我国劳动密集型产品出口竞争力。 出口方
面，首先，1-10 月集成电路及自动数据处理设备出口金额同比实现较快
增速 ， 同比增速分别为111.1%和 60.6%， 海外高技术产品需求仍较旺盛。 
其次，当期机械设备出口表现较好，通用设备、船舶及汽车（和底盘）
出口同比增速分别为 99.5%、27.7%和 52.2 %；值得注意的是，汽车相关
产品出口额在近年出口较高基数的基础上，维持较快增速，我国汽车行
业出口优势仍较明显。 
此外，劳动密集型产品出口仍相对弱势， 1-10 月多数轻工业产品出口金
额同比增速低于当期出口同比增速整体水平，鞋靴、灯具及家用陶瓷器
皿出口同比负增长；我们认为，运价等成本升高或继续影响我国劳动密
集型产品出口竞争力。 
 进口方面，1-10 月，我国集成电路和自动数据处理设备及其零部件同比
分别增长 65.1%和 136.7%。 
 风险提示： 海外大宗商品价格震荡；地缘关系的不确定性； 政策效果不
及预期。

### Page 2

2026 年 11 月 10 日 1-10 月进出口数据点评 2

图表 1.进出口数据表现

资料来源：Wind，中银证券

11 月 10 日，海关总署公布数据显示，以美元计价，2026 年 1-10 月，我国货物贸易出口金额（以下
简称：“出口”）同比增长 22.7%，增幅（较 1-9 月，下同）走扩 1.4 个百分点；货物贸易进口金额
（以下简称：“进口”）同比增长 29.0%，增幅走扩 1.5 个百分点；当期贸易顺差 10425.9 亿美元。
以人民币计价，1-10 月我国出口同比增长 17.6%，进口同比增长 23.6%；贸易顺差为 71632.6 亿元。 
单月数据方面，以美元计价，10 月我国出口同比增长 35.2%，增速较 9 月小幅放缓 1.1 个百分点；
10 月进口同比增长 41.6%，增幅较 9 月明显走扩 10.6 个百分点；贸易顺差 1052.8 亿美元。以人民币
计价，10 月我国出口同比增长 28.7%，增速较 9 月收窄 0.9 个百分点；进口同比增长 34.7%，增速明
显加快 10.1 个百分点。从单月环比数据看，10 月我国出口当月环比下降 7.8%；进口当月环比下降
2.2%。受到国庆假期的影响，10 月出口、进口环比有所下滑，但同比增速仍维持较高水平，我国外
贸景气度维持扩张。 
高技术产业继续支撑海外需求。首先，10 月欧元区、美国 PMI 数据分别为 52.7%和 55.5%，均仍处
于较高景气度区间；10 月韩国、越南当月出口同比增速分别为 93.7%和 53.8%，反映出当前海外需
求的较强韧性，带动我国出口表现。其次，高技术制造业需求维持强韧， 支撑我国出口同比增速表
现。得益于全球“AI”等高技术制造业投资旺盛，10 月当月我国部分机电产品出口同比继续较快增
长，集成电路出口当月同比增速为 137.1%，增速较 9 月加快 1.1 百分点，仍维持较明显的正增长，
全球高技术产业加速发展有望对外需持续形成支撑。 
此外，高技术制造业或也对我国进口表现有所支撑；9 月高技术产业工业增加值同比增长 15.3%，维
持较高水平增速；10 月高技术制造业 PMI 指数 51.3%，仍维持在扩张区间；国内高技术产业增长带
动相关产品进口增速表现，10 月我国集成电路进口当月同比增速为 78.3%，增速较 9 月加快 3.6 个
百分点。 
主要贸易方对我国当月出口同比增速的正贡献均有收窄。分国别看，1-10 月我国对美国、欧盟、东
盟 、 日本及非洲等贸易伙伴的出口同比增速均为正 ， 其中对东盟和非洲的出口同比增速分别为 30.5%
和 24.0%，“一带一路”国家对我国外需的支撑仍较明显；此外，同期对美出口同比增速为12.1%，
增幅较 1-9 月走扩 2.3 个百分点。 
从单月数据表现看 ，10 月东盟进出口总额实现 1240.8 亿美元 ， 占比17.2%； 其中 ， 出口同比增长 46.3%，
进口同比增长 42.0%；10 月我国对欧盟进出口总额 758.0 亿美元，占比 10.5%；其中，出口同比增长
24.3%， 进口同比下滑 4.2%；对美国进出口总额 601.2 亿美元 ， 占比8.4%， 其中 ， 出口同比增长 32.9%，
进口同比增长 35.0%。从贡献角度看，10 月东盟、欧盟及美国对当期出口同比增速的正贡献分别为
8.1、3.5 和 3.8 个百分点；10 月以上主要贸易方对我国出口同比增速的正贡献均有缩窄，东盟、欧
盟及美国正贡献分别收窄 0.4、0.2 和 0.4 个百分点。

### Page 3

2026 年 11 月 10 日 1-10 月进出口数据点评 3

图表 2. 1-10 月主要产品的出口同比增速  图表 3. 1-10 月主要产品的进口同比增速

资料来源：Wind，中银证券  资料来源：Wind，中银证券

运价等成本升高或继续影响我国劳动密集型产品出口竞争力 。出口方面，首先，1-10 月集成电路及
自动数据处理设备出口金额同比实现较快增速，同比增速分别为 111.1%和 60.6%，海外高技术产品
需求仍较旺盛。 
其次，当期机械设备出口表现较好，通用设备、船舶及汽车（和底盘） 出口同比增速分别为99.5%、
27.7%和 52.2 %； 值得注意的是 ，汽车相关产品出口额在近年出口较高基数的基础上 ，维持较快增速，
我国汽车行业出口优势仍较明显。 
此外，劳动密集型产品出口仍相对弱势，1-10 月多数轻工业产品出口金额同比增速低于当期出口同
比增速整体水平，鞋靴、灯具及家用陶瓷器皿出口同比负增长；我们认为，运价等成本升高或继续
影响我国劳动密集型产品出口竞争力。 
进口方面 ， 1-10 月 ， 我国集成电路和自动数据处理设备及其零部件同比分别增长65.1%和 136.7%。

图表 4. 1-10 月主要出口产品价格同比增速表现   图表 5. 1-10 月主要进口产品价格同比增速表现

资料来源：Wind，中银证券  资料来源：Wind，中银证券

价格因素对 10 月出口同比增速的影响仍较明显。1-10 月我国高技术产品进、出口相对活跃，集成电
路进出口均维持较高的累计同比增速；而从价格表现来看，1-10 月集成电路进口、出口价格同比增
速分别为 58.6%和 107.9%，得益于海外、国内高技术产业投资持续发力，我国部分高技术产品进、
出口量价齐升。 
原材料方面，当前中东地区地缘政治冲突仍有反复，对国内上游产品进口需求有所影响。 据我们测
算，1-10 月原油 、成品油进口单价同比增速均高于 15.0%， 但两项产品进口数量同比降幅均高于 15.0%；
此外，同期铜矿砂（及其精矿）和未锻造的铜（及铜材） 的进口单价同比分别增长32.2%和 36.6%，
但以上两种产品同期进口数量也同比下滑，国内工业特别是制造业上游采购需求或仍受到影响。 
风险提示：海外大宗商品价格震荡；地缘关系的不确定性；政策效果不及预期。

披露声明 
本报告准确表述了证券分析师的个人观点。该证券分析师声明，本人未在公司内、外部机构兼任有损本人独立性与客
观性的其他职务，没有担任本报告评论的上市公司的董事、监事或高级管理人员；也不拥有与该上市公司有关的任何
财务权益；本报告评论的上市公司或其它第三方都没有或没有承诺向本人提供与本报告有关的任何补偿或其它利益。  
中银国际证券股份有限公司同时声明，将通过公司网站披露本公司授权公众媒体及其他机构刊载或者转发证券研究报
告有关情况。 如有投资者于未经授权的公众媒体看到或从其他机构获得本研究报告的， 请慎重使用所获得的研究报告，
以防止被误导，中银国际证券股份有限公司不对其报告理解和使用承担任何责任。  
评级体系说明 
以报告发布日后公司股价/行业指数涨跌幅相对同期相关市场指数的涨跌幅的表现为基准： 
公司投资评级： 
买    入：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内超越基准指数 20%以上； 
增    持：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内超越基准指数 10%-20%； 
中    性：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内相对基准指数变动幅度在-10%-10%之间； 
减    持：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内相对基准指数跌幅在 10%以上； 
未有评级：因无法获取必要的资料或者其他原因，未能给出明确的投资评级。

行业投资评级： 
强于大市：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现强于基准指数； 
中    性：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现基本与基准指数持平； 
弱于大市：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现弱于基准指数； 
未有评级：因无法获取必要的资料或者其他原因，未能给出明确的投资评级。

沪深市场基准指数为沪深 300 指数；新三板市场基准指数为三板成指或三板做市指数；香港市场基准指数为恒生指数
或恒生中国企业指数；美股市场基准指数为纳斯达克综合指数或标普 500 指数。

## Document 045: 20261106【投研報告】市場快訊_資料中心監管疑慮衝擊AI供應鏈，美股全面重挫

不另行通知。
資料中心監管疑慮衝擊 AI 供應鏈，美股全面重挫
2026 年 11 月 06 日
一、 市場動態
1. 資料中心監管疑慮引爆 AI 供應鏈賣壓，美股選後漲勢一日反轉：民主黨取得參、眾兩
院多數，美國進入分裂政府格局。S&P 500 於 11 月 4 日先漲 1.58%，11 月 5 日卻重
挫 4.36%，創2025 年 4 月以來最大單日跌幅 ；Nasdaq 跌 5.52%，費半大跌 12.25%，
十一大產業全數收黑。主因有三：一是資料中心開發成為州級政策議題，德州州長當
選人主張暫停新建，全美新增容量約三成可能受影響；二是高通與 Arm 財報不如預

期；三是建設遞延將推高依賴外部融資業者的持有成本。費半重挫前已自 6 月高點回
落近三成，政策不確定性進一步放大跌幅（詳見表一）。
表一：美股主要指數表現（2026/11/05 收盤）

Nasdaq 綜合指數 24,315.00 −5.52%
費城半導體指數 9,011.14 −12.25%

中升至 28.09（前日 16.61）。資金湧入美債，2 年、5 年、10 年及 30 年期殖利率分
別降至 4.46%、4.59%、4.84%及 5.25%，呈牛市陡峭化；12 月升息一碼機率由約
83%降至約 65%。布蘭特原油反彈 3.88%至每桶 98.29 美元，通膨前景仍具不確定性
（詳見表二） 。

二、 下跌原因
1. 民主黨橫掃國會及州長選舉，資料中心開發成為州級政策議題：德州州長當選人 Gina
Hinojosa 主張暫停新建資料中心，俄亥俄州與喬治亞州當選人也提出限制。地方政府

可透過電價分攤、建設許可及稅務優惠影響新案進度，UBS 估計 2028 年全美新增資
料中心容量約三成可能受影響。國會對科技巨頭與資料中心用電的監督亦可能增強，
半導體、電力設備及資料中心類股成為賣壓重心。
2. 高通手機晶片業績低於預期，加重半導體類股賣壓：高通財年第四季調整後 EPS 年減
28%至 2.17 美元，手機晶片營收年減 26%至 51.37 億美元，低於預期，主因蘋果相
關收入減少；汽車與物聯網營收雖年增 36%，仍難抵銷缺口，股價隔日收跌 9.5%，
進一步放大半導體跌幅。
3. 融資成本與投資回收壓力加重，依賴外部融資業者首當其衝：資料中心資本支出多發

生於上線前，若電力、許可或工程延後，收入認列隨之推遲，利息與持有成本卻須持
續負擔。儘管 12 月升息機率降至約 65%，借款成本仍高，依賴外部融資或單一客戶
合約的 AI 基礎建設業者將面臨回報壓縮與再融資風險。
4. 科技股在本次重挫前已明顯修正 ， 評價落至極低水準：費半自 6 月 22 日高點 14,634.72
點已回落逾 30%，Nasdaq 亦自 10 月 6 日高點 27,599.89 點回落逾 10%。市場質疑
AI 投資效益，降低對高評價企業的容忍度，政策不確定性因而放大修正。近期跌幅較
屬估值下調而非基本面惡化，若晶片訂單與獲利預估維持穩定，市場有望回溫。

三、 市場看法
1. 短期評價修正而非長期趨勢反轉，AI 資本循環仍處擴張階段：我們視本波為多頭格局
中的健康修正。修正反映股東對龐大資本承諾要求更高風險補償，而非 AI 變現失速：
Google Cloud 第三季營收年增 103%、 營業利益年增209%，四大雲端業者資本支出
預估上修至約 1.16 兆美元，顯示企業看見更大市場而主動加碼（詳見表四）。此外，
政策衝擊集中於州與地方，聯邦仍支持資料中心擴建，選舉結果本質是要求用電成本
內部化，而非否定 AI 投資。

Google Cloud 第三季營業利益 年增 209% 獲利能力未因擴張而惡化

不另行通知。 
2. 留意五大反彈訊號，利空逐步消化後市場可望回穩：（一）州級電價與併網制度若於
2027 年上半年 建立 ，延後 專案可望 下半年重新加速 ，眾議院 以 417 比 3 通過
《Ratepayer Protection Act》亦顯示跨黨派共識；（二）雲端業者資本支出指引未下
修、未見取消訂單；（三）11 月 10 日 CPI 及 13 日 PPI 物價月增率回落；（四）財政
部維持附息債標售規模，資金回流市場；（五）VIX 回落且投資等級債利差穩定。訊號
出現後，市場有望回歸基本面。 
3. 政策先影響建設速度，AI 需求仍須以訂單驗證：州級限制首先改變的是資料中心選址
與交付時程，而非 AI 需求本身。雲端客戶若維持支出計畫，專案可調整順序或轉往他
州，惟設備交付遞延將壓抑供應商近季營收；聯邦立法限制則須經國會及總統否決權，
短期落地難度高。若主要客戶訂單穩定，本次修正較屬建設時程遞延的風險重定價。 
4. 新增算力面臨遞延，既有算力相對有利：高盛估計可能收緊開發限制的州約占美國規
劃中資料中心容量的 45%，其中德州約占 25%。新案放慢下，大型雲端業者既有算力
可望受惠於利用率與議價能力提升，資本支出延後亦減輕現金流壓力；仍待併網的專
案及高槓桿業者則面臨回收遞延，產業鏈分化將擴大。 
5. 歷史經驗顯示選後股市多正向，後續表現仍宜留意基本面變化：彭博統計 1950 年以
來，美國期中選舉後 12 個月 S&P 500 平均報酬約 15%，19 次全數收正，最高為 1954
年的逾 30%，最弱的 1986 年亦小幅上漲。府會分治抑制政策大幅轉向為選後行情主
要支撐，惟後續表現仍須觀察基本面(詳見下圖)。

6. 企業獲利仍強勁成長，本次修正以估值與風險溢價重估為主：彭博 11 月 4 日統計，
S&P 500 已公布財報公司中 79.85%優於預期，資訊科技更達 92.45%，第三季 EPS
成長率分別為 29.0%及 67.8%（詳見表五）。財報為美股提供中期支撐，短期評價則
取決於 2027 年獲利預估能否承受建設遞延與融資成本壓力。 
表五：S&P 500 第三季財報統計（截至 2026/11/04）

7. 最新經濟數據仍具韌性，聯準會利率路徑仍具變數：初領失業救濟金降至 18.9 萬人，
第三季勞動生產力季增年率 3.6%、單位勞動成本季減 0.7%，經濟仍具韌性。惟油價
逼近每桶 100 美元，若通膨高於預期，升息定價恐再升溫，壓抑科技股估值與長債價
格。
8. 信用市場是判斷風險是否由股權評價擴散至金融條件的重要指標：若 AI 企業持續擴大
資本支出與外部融資，償債能力將受關注。大型投資等級科技企業現金流穩定，較能

吸收壓力；高槓桿或依賴單一客戶的 AI 基礎建設業者則較易受利差上升衝擊。信用利
差若維持穩定，風險仍以股權評價調整為主；若同步擴大，則須警惕金融條件收緊。
四、 投資建議：
1. 短期修正不改 AI 長期趨勢 ， 切勿恐慌殺低錯殺優質標的：本次重挫源自選後政策不確
定性與評價重新定價，AI 需求與企業獲利尚未反轉，屬多頭中的短期波動。應避免恐
慌出清而錯殺優質標的；已持有者以續抱為主，加碼宜分批逢低，避免高槓桿操作。
2. 以股債平衡穿越短期波動，等待基本面重新主導市場：我們認為，本次修正屬短期政
策衝擊與評價調整，中長期科技股展望仍正面。在政策與通膨明朗前，建議將集中度
過高的科技股部位，適度轉向波動較低的平衡型或多重資產商品，搭配優質債券穩定
收益，並保留股票部位分批布局優質標的，待反彈訊號確認後再提高風險資產比重。
3. 股票部位聚焦優質、評價回落且前景較佳的產業：修正後部分優質企業評價已回到合
理區間，股票建議聚焦：一、握有長期電力合約、已通電園區的大型雲端業者；二、
明年展望佳的 AI 供應鏈，如 ASIC、光通訊、電力設備、半導體設備及資安；三、跌

幅較小的軟體及非科技優質股。高度仰賴新建資料中心與外部融資的業者，宜待環境
明朗再評估。
4. 中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債仍較具優勢：在美國政策風險升溫及科技股評價
調整期間，美國公債仍具投資組合避險功能，但考量通膨與能源價格的不確定性，現
階段不建議延長存續期間。中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債在兼顧票息收益、利
率敏感度與資產配置穩定性上仍較具優勢。信用債方面，營運現金流充足、槓桿適中
及再融資需求可控的發行人仍較為穩健，對AI 相關企業則須進一步評估資本支出、合
約現金流與債務到期結構。

海外產業訊息評論 
美國首家純人型機器人公司 Agility Robotics(AGLT US)上市看法 
商業化前景可期，高評價難掩獲利風險

報告日期：2026.11.9 研究員：吳俞潔 
11 月 5 日美國首家純人形機器人公司 Agility Robotics（AGLT US） 掛牌上
市， 公司透過與特殊目的收購公司Churchill Capital（CCXI US） 合併上市，
上市前股權估值達 25 億。Agility 旗下 Digit 人形機器人已經投入 GXO、
Schaeffler 及 Toyota 的實際作業，累積超過 6.5 萬小時運作經驗，新一代
Digit 5 也取得超過 3 億的多年期訂單， 並且鴻海領投 2 億 PIPE 融資 ，Nvidia
則提供 Digit 5 所需的 IGX Thor 運算平台及資安技術，市場看好 Agility 有
機會率先實現人形機器人大規模商業化，因此這次上市及相關融資募得 4.65
億，也讓市場開始關注人形機器人由技術展示轉向商業化後的成長空間。 
不過，兆豐認為 Agility 雖然率先將人形機器人導入外部企業客戶的實際作
業，並且取得多年期訂單，但目前營收規模非常有限，26H1 營收僅 17.4 萬
(年減 72%)，營業虧損則達 3.63 億，而且超過 3 億訂單為跨年度承諾，須符
合產品規格及交付條件才能如期轉化為實際營收。以 25 億上市前股權估值
來看，市場已經提前反映未來數年大規模部署的成長空間，但 Digit 5 預計
27H1 才開始交付早期客戶，2027 年底才全面上市，現階段難確認實際出貨
速度、單台成本 、服務合約現金回收期 及長期獲利能力 ，因此兆豐目前對
Agility Robotics（AGLT US）持中性看待。

Agility 搶先特斯拉取得商業客戶，人形機器人開始進入實際工作場域 
Agility 成立於 2015 年 ， 主要開發應用於物流 、 製造及倉儲的人形機器人Digit，
相較特斯拉 Optimus 以通用型人形機器人及大規模生產為發展方向，Agility
搶先切入物流搬運、物料處理相關重複性高的工業作業，利用人形機器人能
適應既有廠房及倉儲環境的特性，降低企業重新設計工作場域的需求。並且
採用機器人即服務(RaaS)模式 ， 透過長期合約提供Digit 及配套的維護服務 ，
並且搭配 Agility Arc 軟體管理機器人運作，讓企業客戶不必一次負擔全部設
備採購成本，也讓 Agility 有機會隨著客戶部署數量增加，取得持續性的服務
收入。 
目前 Agility 已經和 GXO、Schaeffler 及 Toyota 的加拿大製造工廠合作， 
GXO 於 2024 年在美國物流中心正式部署 Digit，近期再宣布 2027 年初擴大
至另外兩座物流中心，Toyota 則簽署 RaaS 商業協議以及農業器具公司愛科
的商業試點合約。相比之下，特斯拉在這季宣布Optimus 於佛利蒙廠產線開

### Page 2

評等分級：買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進 （預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操作
（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出 （預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間） ；賣出 （預期
未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。

海外產業訊息評論 
始生產，但處於初期量產階段，因此 Agility 目前的優勢在於已經取得企業付
費使用經驗，並能利用實際作業資料改善產品性能，不過客戶從測試轉向正
式採購需要時間，目前已經正式部署的客戶數量及規模也相當有限。

3 億訂單提高成長能見度，但 25 億估值已經提前反映量產規模 
Agility 在上市前取得超過 3 億的 Digit 5 多年期訂單 ， 主要來自單一客戶1,000
台機器人及三年期 RaaS 合約，以 3 億及 1,000 台估算，平均每台對應三年
合約價值 30 萬，年化 10 萬(包含服務期間的合約價值)。Digit 5 預計 27H1
開始交付早期客戶 ，2027 年底全面上市 ， 因此未來營收取決於實際交付數量
及合約履行進度，短期難以直接反映在財報。 
公司於美國奧勒岡州建置 RoboFab 工廠，設計年產能最高達 1 萬台，並計
劃在 2027 年底前將潛在客戶由 30 多家提高至 100 家，承諾部署的客戶據
點由 9 處增加至 25 處。不過，目前 1 萬台僅為工廠充分運作後的設計年產
能 ， 並非實際出貨量， 而30 多家潛在客戶也尚未全部轉化為正式訂單，現有
超過 3 億訂單集中在單一客戶，未來能否取得其他大型客戶，並且將測試計
畫轉為客戶正式部署，將直接影響產能利用率及營收成長速度。 
兆豐認為目前市場對 Agility 的期待，主要來自人形機器人有機會取代部分勞
力密集工作，以及公司率先取得外部商業訂單。不過，公司 2025 年全年營
收僅 178 萬，26H1 更降至 17.4 萬，以上市前 25 億股權估值對應 2025 年
營收 178 萬，P/S 高達 1,404 倍，目前估值提前大幅反映了未來數年營收成
長，雖然公司取得超過 3 億的多年期訂單，但集中於單一客戶，Digit 5 預計
2027 年才開始擴大交付，對於現階段 Agility 來說，高評價必須仰賴未來訂
單持續增加及量產進度符合預期，若 2027 年出貨或客戶拓展不如預期，將
有大幅下修空間。

量產及營運成本高，人形機器人商業化效益有待驗證 
雖然 Agility 已經開始在工廠及物流中心實際部署 Digit， 但兆豐認為人形機器
人能否大規模商業化，關鍵在於使用成本是否低於現有人力或傳統自動化設
備，目前工業自動化已有機械手臂、無人搬運車及自主移動機器人相關成熟
方案， 因此部分固定、重複性高的工作不一定需要透過人形機器人執行。
Agility 必須證明 Digit 除了能適應既有工作環境 ， 也能透過長時間穩定運作及
較低的維護成本，為企業帶來足夠的經濟效益，才能提高客戶採購意願。  
從公司本身來看，RaaS 雖然能降低客戶初期導入門檻，但 Agility 必須先負
擔機器人製造及部署成本，再透過後服務合約逐步回收資金。根據公司上市
材料提到，Digit 初期單台材料成本 15 萬，隨著年產量達 1,000 台/ 1 萬台，
成本分別有機會降至 7.5 萬/ 3 萬，但這些仍然是公司假設，實際達成取決於
產能利用率、零組件成本及製造效率。 
Agility 認列 26H1 營收僅 17.4 萬(年減 72%)，營業虧損達 3.63 億，而且超
過 3 億訂單為跨年度承諾，須符合產品規格及交付條件才能如期轉化為實際
營收 。 以25 億上市前股權估值來看 ， 市場已經提前反映未來數年大規模部署

### Page 3

Alchip (3661.TW)
07 November 2026 Citi Research
3
AI infrastructure demand, supply constraints and 
engineering productivity are key for growth outlook
Despite rising concerns over data center power availability, infrastructure 
deployment delays and potential AI investment moderation, management reported 
no meaningful slowdown in customer demand or changes in development 
schedules. Based on discussions with major customers, Alchip expects strong 
demand extending through 2027–2029, with next-generation AI accelerator 
volumes potentially exceeding current-generation levels substantially. 
Management cautioned that shipment delays could arise from supply-side 
constraints, particularly PCB, substrates, passive components and advanced 
semiconductor capacity, rather than weakening end demand. More importantly,  
large AI accelerator projects generally receive priority allocation, leaving smaller 
customers and traditional applications more exposed to shortages. Meanwhile, AI 
adoption is materially improving Alchip’s engineering productivity, particularly in 
RTL coding, verification, design iteration and analysis of implementation reports. 
Alchip’s CPU and networking projects are progressing on schedule, but 
management believes advanced AI accelerator designs offer substantially greater 
engineering differentiation and revenue opportunities than conventional CPU 
projects.
Looking into 2028, the key limitation could shift from customer demand to Alchip’s 
ability to finance and support larger wafer procurement commitments. 
Consequently, working-capital requirements, customer payment terms and the 
potential need for additional financing will become increasingly important factors 
in determining the company’s achievable revenue scale and profitability. 
We lift our 2027 /2028 earnings projection for Alchip by 14% and 13%, reflecting 
stronger order allocation into next two years. We are maintaining our Buy on the 
stock but lowering our TP to NT$6,000, with lower PER at 27x of 2027 EPS (from 
35x previously), reflecting macro and AI policy uncertainty, in the low end of its 
historical PER.

### Page 7

Alchip (3661.TW)
07 November 2026 Citi Research
7
Alchip
Company description
Alchip is a leading provider of silicon design and manufacturing service,
specializing in ASIC (Application-Specific Integrated Circuit) design,
development, and production. Founded in 2003, the company has >1,000
employees and operates in Taipei, Shanghai, Japan, Korea, and the US. Alchip
serves a wide range of industries, including automotive, industrials, consumer
electronics, and computing.

Investment strategy
We rate Alchip as Buy. We expect Alchip to deliver a strong snapback in
2026/27 with its N3 project ramping up. Moreover, we are also positive over
the long-term growth opportunity from its AI ASIC projects with its major CSP
client. Alchip remains one of the few design service providers within TSMC’s
3DFabric alliance. In our view, if CSPs work more aggressively on their internal
design and become more competitive on product offerings, Alchip would be

well positioned as a back-end/turnkey partner.
Valuation
Our target price for Alchip of NT$6,000 is based on 27x 2027E EPS. 27x target
PER is lower than its historical 3-year PER range, which we see as justified on
account of strong growth potential of its AI ASIC projects with its key CSP
client, reflecting macro and AI policy uncertainty but with better project cycle
and strong growth momentum resuming next year. Our target price equates
to 2026/27E P/B of 6x/5x.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

## Document 134: 261108_ms_novatek

Source PDF: all report pdf/261108_ms_novatek.pdf
Report source: line報告備份

### Page 1

M
Update
Novatek  | Asia Pacific
Consumer Demand Softens as
Cost Pressure Builds
3Q growth led by smartphones: Revenue was NT$30.97bn, +8% Q/Q and +26% Y/
Y, 7% above MSe. Net income was NT$6.08bn, -4% Q/Q and +66% Y/Y, 2% above
MSe, with EPS of NT$9.99. Smartphone restocking lifted sales, but GM fell 1.53ppt
Q/Q to 40.89% on a lower SoC mix and a lag in cost pass-through. Lower opex
supported a 9.6% Q/Q rise in operating profit; net income declined in the absence of

2Q's NT$860mn one-off investment gain.
4Q revenue and margins seen declining sequentially: Management guided for
revenue of NT$28.0-29.2bn, down 6-10% Q/Q, with GM of 37-39% and OpM of 17-
19%, assuming NT$/US$32. Large DDICs should face the largest sequential revenue
drop, particularly from notebooks, followed by SoC; small/medium DDICs should
decrease slightly despite stronger Chinese OLED handset orders. Lower-cost
inventory depletion, product mix and higher memory KGD costs will likely weigh on

margins. Prices have been adjusted across SoC and DDIC products, but management
still sees higher memory prices constraining consumer demand into 2027.
New products offer growth, with cost risks unresolved: Automotive eDP TDDI is
scheduled for mass production in 4Q26, while touch-enabled notebook products are
scheduled to ramp through 2027. OLED solutions for IT applications and higher-
performance handset products broaden opportunities, although smartphone OLED

DDIC shipments are expected to decline Y/Y in 2026. Management expects new
products to support 2027 revenue growth but provided no quantified target or
assurance of flat Y/Y margins. Further foundry and OSAT price increases remain
under negotiation, leaving earnings dependent upon product mix and the pace of
cost pass-through.
Morgan Stanley Taiwan Limited+
Daniel Yen, CFA

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Novatek (3034.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity constant at 9.8% (2.0%
risk-free rate, 6% risk premium, 1.3 beta), payout ratio of 80%, medium-term growth rate of
6.1% and terminal growth rate assumption of 3.4%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected 8K/4K TV growth.
n Better-than-expected smartphone sell-through.
n Better-than-expected sell-through of foldable products.

Risks to Downside
n Intensified price competition in TDDI, driving Novatek's gross margin below its corpo-
rate average.
n Slower-than-expected 8K/4K TV growth.
n Slower sell-through of foldable products.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 226: [005380] 현대차(005380) 저점을 받쳐줄 실적현대차(005380) 저점을 받쳐줄 실적저점을 받쳐줄 실적 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005380] 현대차(005380) 저점을 받쳐줄 실적현대차(005380) 저점을 받쳐줄 실적저점을 받쳐줄 실적 - 기업.pdf

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 
목표주가 500,000 
하향  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
292,500 
자동차업종

◼ 3Q26 비용절감/환율 효과로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현 
◼ 2027년 물량–환율 영향 상쇄되며 2H27 이익체력 개선 본격화 예상 
◼ 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등에 주목

투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 50만원 하향(-6%) 
현대차 TP 50만원(시총 102조원)은 12MF EPS 41,978원에 Target PER 12배
적용하여 산출. 제한적인 실적 기대 반영해 타겟 밸류 하향함에 따라 TP 조정  
우려 대비 양호한 3Q26 실적으로 주가 하방 부담 완화. 주가의 추세적 상승 
위해서는 1) Physical AI(로봇/SDV) 경쟁력, 2) 주요국 점유율 반등 확인돼야 할 
것. 기저효과에 따른 물량 개선이 주가 저점 받쳐줄 것이나, 현재 밸류(2027년 
PER 9.1배vs 글로벌 피어 6.1배)에서 추세 상승 이끌긴 어려울 것이기 때문. 
이에, 자동차 본업 외 1) 한/미국 Physical AI 투자 계획 및 2) BD 공급망 구축 
/양산 가시화에 따른 주가 반등 기대

3Q26 매출 45조(YoY -3%, 이하 YoY), OP 2.7조(+6%), OPM 5.9% 시현 
3Q26 OP 추정(2.5조)/컨센(2.4조) 상회. 파업에 따른 생산/판매 차질 영향으로 
기대 제한적이었으나, 비용 절감/환율 영향으로 우려 대비 양호한 실적 시현. 
[자동차] 매출 33조(-10%), OP 2.1조(+25%), OPM 6.5%. 도매 판매 92.1만대
(-11%). 판매 부진과 원자재 부담에도 불구, 환효과(+1.3조)/비용절감(+0.5조)
로 실적 방어. 이외 YoY 변수별 영향은 물량(-1.1조)/믹스(-0.6조)/원자재(-
0.5조). [금융] 매출 9.2조(+28%), OP 4,710억(-18%), OPM 5.1%, [기타] 매출 
2.9조(+2%), OP 712억(-72%), OPM 2.5% 기록

2027년 매출 191조(YoY +2%, 이하 YoY), OP 12.7조(+15%), OPM 6.6% 전망 
기저효과 바탕으로 연결 도매 물량 YoY +5% 증가 예상하나, 원달러 환율 -
4% 하락(가정 환율 1,380원)하며 1H27 → 2H27로가며 이익체력 개선 예상 
(단위: 십억원, %)

### Page 5

DAISHIN SECURITIES

5  
  현대차 3Q26 실적 컨퍼런스콜 QnA 
Q. 금융부문 환율 변동에 따른 4Q26 매출 및 손익 영향 
- 금융부문 환율 영향은 현대차 실적에 미치는 환율 영향과 동일한 방향으로 발생

Q. 3Q26 금융부문 수익성 악화 요인 
- 장기금리 상승에 따른 조달 및 이자비용 증가가 주요인. 자산/부채 만기 매칭에 따른 
금리 상승 영향 반영 
- EV 중심 중고차 가격지수 소폭 하락 및 차량 반환에 따른 매각손실, 금리 상승에 따
른 연체율 및 신용비용 상승 
- 차량 판매가격 인하 영향은 없으며, 3Q26 도매 판매 감소에 따른 금융 영업수익 영향 
불가피했음

Q. 향후 주주환원 정책에서 배당 및 자사주 매입 우선순위 
- 기존 밸류업 정책: 최소 DPS 1만원/TPR 35% 제시. 연간 배당 약 2.6조원 → TPR 
35% 충족 위한 잔여분 자사주 매입/소각 활용 
- 경영환경 변화 고려하여 기존 밸류업 프로그램 전반의 주주가치 제고 방안 재검토중 
- 새로운 정책 확정 및 주주 소통 전까지 기존 약속인 최소 DPS 1만원 유지할 계획 
- 시장의 배당 및 자사주 매입 간 균형 요구를 인지하고 있으며, 주주 의견 수렴 후 주
주가치 제고 계획 공유 예정

Q. 3Q26 영업이익 증감 요인 중 기타 +6,400억 발생 원인 
- 월별 컨틴전시 활동을 통한 비용 최적화 및 재료비 절감 지속 추진 
- 4Q26 원가 절감 효과는 3Q26 대비 확대 예상

Q. 3Q26 영업외손익 감소 원인 
- 3Q26 환율 급락으로 외화 매출채권 등 외화자산 관련 외화환산손실 및 외환거래손실 
발생 
- 매출채권 인식 시점과 회수 시점 간 환율 차이에 따른 외환차손 발생 
- 지분법 대상 기업들의 3Q26 실적 부진에 따른 지분법손익 감소도 일부 영향. 대부분 
외환 관련 손실에서 발생

Q. 중국 OEM 국내 진출 확대에 따른 대응 및 신차/가격 전략 
- 중국 OEM 및 테슬라의 국내 M/S 확대 대응중. 다만, 잦은 가격 조정은 중고차 잔존
가치 및 고객가치 훼손할 수 있어 지양 
- 현대캐피탈의 캡티브 금융 경쟁력 활용. 잔존가치 유예할부 등을 통해 월 납입금을 낮
춰 중국 OEM 대비 경쟁력 확보 
- 서비스 네트워크 기반으로 경쟁사 대비 경쟁력 확보 노력 
- 국내 신차: 4Q26 아반떼 HEV, 투싼, GV90, G80 HEV, 싼타페 FL(2026.12월), G90 
FL 등 출시 예정 
- 북미: 4Q26 투싼, GV80 HEV 출시 및 27년 초 GV90 출시 예상 
- 유럽: 아이오닉3 판매 개시, 4Q26 투싼/베이온 등 주요 모델 출시 예정 
- 중국: 아이오닉 V / 인도: 베이온, 아이오닉 P 등 출시 예정 
- 신차 출시 계획 연도별로 다수 예정. 가격은 원가/경쟁사/소비자 가격 수용성 등을 고
려해 결정

### Page 6

현대차(005380)

Q. 3Q26 법인세비용 증가 원인 및 4Q26 전망 
- 일회성 요인보다는 법인별 손익 믹스 및 적용 세율 차이에 따른 영향 
- 4Q26 법인세 수준은 QoQ 큰 변동 없을 것으로 전망

## Document 256: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발적HD현대중공업(329180) 실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발_5484f7f5

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발적HD현대중공업(329180) 실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발_5484f7f5.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
실적&임단협 우려 해소, 발전엔진 수요 폭발적 
2026-10-29

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 640,000 원(하향) 
현재주가: 285,500 원 
상승여력: 124.2%

Signal: 3Q26, 조업일수 감소 및 환율 하락으로 시장 예상치 하회 
Key: 4Q26~1Q27부터 본격적인 발전엔진 및 FDC 수주 모멘텀 전망 
Step: 상선 우려 감안해도 엔진&FDC으로 밸류 매력도 충분. 매수 기회

3Q26 Review: 시장예상치 하회하는 실적 시현  
3Q26 연결 매출액 5조 7,536억원(+30.2% YoY, -9.1% QoQ), 영업이익 8,992억원
(+61.3% YoY, -13.5% QoQ)으로, 시장 예상치(9,792억원)를 하회했다. 조업일수 감소
(휴가 10일+중대재해 4일) 및 환율 하락 영향(450억원)과 더불어, 임단협 관련 예상 비
용 중 75%를 이번 분기 선반영 했기 때문이다. 상선 수주 빈티지는 중형 선박 기준 ‘23년 
9.6%, ‘24년 65%, ‘25년 25%, 대형 선박 기준 ‘22년 4%, ‘23년 45%, ‘24년 31%, 
‘25년 20%로, 선가 상승효과 지속되는 중이다. 특히 해양 부문에서 루야 프로젝트의 공정
률 확대 효과 및 C/O로 180억원의 환입 발생으로 전 분기 대비 큰 폭의 이익 성장 시현했
다. 최근 키윗과 미국 내 합작 조선소 설립을 추진하며, 미 군수지원함 초도 2척 수주 및 향
후 물량 건조, 그리고 HII와의 함정 선체블록 하도급 사업 수주까지 기대할 수 있다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 64 만원으로 하향 
HD현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’를 유지하고, 목표주가는 기존 82만원에서 64만원
으로 하향한다. 최근 상선 실적 피크아웃 우려에 따른 조선주 주가 급락으로 멀티플 하향 조
정했다. 하지만 상선 부문의 우려를 감안하더라도 상선 사이클과 무관한 육상 발전 엔진 및 
FDC에 대한 글로벌 하이퍼스케일러들의 수요 확대는 지속되고 있는 중이다. 올해 공시 기
준으로 육상발전 엔진 약 1.6GW 수주한 것으로 알려졌으나, 미공시된 약 1.4GW까지 포
함 시 총 3.0GW 이상을 수주한 상황이다. 캐파 증설 기준으로 ‘28 년 1.5GW, ‘29 년 
3GW 캐파에 해당하는 물량을 이미 대부분 채웠으며, 기존 계획 대비 빠른 준공 혹은 공정
률 확대, 더 나아가 추가적인 캐파 증설까지 고려할 수준으로 파악된다. 빠르면 올해 연말 
혹은 내년 초부터 4 행정 중속엔진&FDC 본격 수주를 통해 중장기적 신성장 동력 확보할 
전망이다. 현 주가 레벨에서 상승여력 충분히 남아있다는 판단으로, 3Q 실적 및 임단협 불
확성 해소된 현 시점에서 적극적 매수 의견 및 조선주 최선호주(Top-Pick)로 제시한다.

TUẦN 12-16/10 [Điểm nhấn thị trường] 
VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh 
khoản suy giảm

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
VN-Index có thể hồi kỹ thuật từ cạnh dưới tam giác, song việc chớm thủng hỗ trợ 
1.710 điểm và số mã còn giữ xu hướng tăng từ đáy tháng 7 suy gi ảm khiến xu 
hướng ngắn hạn trở nên trung tính.

### Page 2

2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Dầu khí, Bảo hiểm và Dịch vụ phân phối là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -3,013 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã MCH, VIC và FPT; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 
887 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -2,021 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -4,384 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng -
4,275 hợp đồng và đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -7,815 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam tiếp tục điều chỉnh tuần thứ tư liên tiếp, khi VN-Index lùi về đóng cửa 1.706 
điểm - giảm khoảng 1,7% so với tuần trước và đang dao động quanh vùng hỗ trợ tâm lý 
1.700 điểm, cũng là vùng đáy cũ của tháng 8. Thanh khoản thị trường tiếp tục suy yếu, 
phản ánh tâm lý thận trọng cao độ trong bối cảnh thị trường thiếu vắng thông tin hỗ trợ 
trong nước, trong khi các yếu tố bên ngoài như xung đột tại Iran, và chi phí vốn toàn cầu 
chưa hạ nhiệt tiếp tục đè nặng tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Khối ngoại duy trì chuỗi bán ròng kéo 
dài nhiều phiên liên tiếp, nối dài đà rút vốn đã lên tới xấp xỉ 95 nghìn tỷ đồng từ đầu năm. 
Trong một, hai tuần tới, mùa báo cáo kết quả kinh doanh quý 3 được kỳ vọng là chất xúc 
tác giúp thị trường giao dịch cân bằng trở lại.

Trung hạn (Từ 3 đến 9 tháng)

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tiếp tục hình thành mẫu nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên đồ thị tuần, đánh dấu tuần 
giảm thứ 4 liên tiếp, cho thấy áp lực phân phối vẫn đang có phần lấn át. Trên khung ngày, 
tuy chỉ số đã có nỗ lực phục hồi trở lại trong 3 phiên đan xen, đặc biệt là phản ứng trong 
phiên cuối tuần khi chạm trendline trung hạn, nhưng việc chỉ có thể hình thành các mẫu 
nến "spinning" hàm ý đà tích lũy chưa thực sự thuyết phục. Trong một vài phiên kế tiếp, 
VNINDEX có thể xuất hiện nhịp hồi kỹ thuật từ vùng hỗ trợ, tương ứng với cạnh dưới tam 
giác, nhưng sau khi chớm phá vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1710 và hiện chỉ còn một số ít các mã giữ 
được xu hướng tăng tính từ đáy tháng 7, xu hướng ngắn hạn đang trở nên khá trung tính.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1780 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1740 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1680 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1630 (+-15)

lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng.

- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

前日收盤價                              76 .6元

許哲豪 
Jeff.syu@ctbcsis.com

GIS-KY(6456 TT)       法說 /座談摘要  
雷射封裝業務進展順利， 2Q27有望顯著放量  
⚫ 公司積極轉型自傳統觸控模組轉向半導體光學  
⚫ 雷射封測試量產進行中，預期年底出貨， 2027將有顯著貢獻  
結論： GIS-KY 3Q26營收有起色，但仍受終端市場需求不振及客戶端記
憶體缺貨的影響，但整體財務結構與淨現金部位 仍穩健。公司目前正積
極推動業務轉型，從傳統觸控模組轉向半導體光學領域，並已擴大資本
支出佈局光通訊雷射光源封測與 AR/VR等新產品，預計這些新事業將於
2027至 2028年成為公司顯著的營收成長動能 。

3Q26財務數據  
⚫ 營收 144億元(QoQ+37.6%，YoY-10.5%)，主要受到終端市場需求
不振及客戶端記憶體缺貨的影響。  
⚫ 毛利率 4.02%(QoQ+0.68ppts，YoY-2.46ppts)  
⚫ 營業費用 12.11億元，前三季單季皆維持在 11.2億元左右的水準。
因公司處於轉型期，於 R&D的投資相對較多。  
⚫ 營業利益 -6.3億元 
⚫ 稅前淨利 -6.69億元 
⚫ 單季 EPS -3.15元 
⚫ 稅後淨利 -7.25億元 
⚫ 單季 EPS -2.17元。 
產品營收占比  
⚫ 3Q26：平板佔 40%、筆電佔 28%、指紋辨識模組佔 13%、其他佔
19%。 
⚫ 1Q-3Q26：平板佔 32%、筆電佔 21%、指紋辨識模組佔 9%、其他
佔 38%。 
法說會內容摘要  
1.  公司中長期轉型策略：半導體光學 (Semi Optics)  
• 光學元件：主要應用於 AR/VR眼鏡、 Waveguide及抬頭顯示器
（HUD） ，涵蓋奈米壓印、蝕刻及鍍膜等製程，目前擁有多個國際大廠
專案。預期 AR/VR軟硬體需至 2027至 2028年才會成熟發酵。  
• 雷射封裝：為近期資本支出重點方向，主力產品為 CW Laser CoC
以及未來的 VCSEL VOD。

### Page 2

【中信投顧投資分析報告】 
個股摘要

202 6 /11 /10

2

• 光通訊元件：發展被動光學元件，不排除整合 FAU、Metalens及上下游模零組件整合。  
• Micro OLED / Micro LED：Micro OLED在顯示器應用發展符合預期，並積極研發 Micro 
LED在光通訊領域的應用。

Q&A內容摘要  
1. Q：新世代半導體光學產品的資本支出目前的廠區規劃及進度 為何？後續大客戶的營
收比重是否會下降？   
A：公告約 80億元的資本支出中，雷射封裝相關預計佔 40至 50億元，約三分之一投入台
灣廠區，其餘投入海外越南新廠。剩下的資本支出將用於 Micro LED、AR/VR波導及大客
戶新產品（廠區分散於大陸及台灣）。與大陸大客戶合作近 20年互信基礎深厚，只要有觸
控與顯示的新產品皆會參與，大客戶仍是重要客戶，但隨新業務逐步發酵，其營收佔比的
確會漸漸下降。

2. Q：10月份營收跳升破百億元的原因？這是一次性的嗎？這是否會佔用過多營運資
金？  
A：10月營收達 130億元，主因為客戶委託進行手機關鍵零組件的加工與精密對位貼合，
這是相對長期的專案，並非一次性。營收跳升主力來自高單價材料，若純看加工費，實際
營收佔比並不高。材料確實會佔用較大營運資金，但因週期短，銀行額度支持無虞；先前
提出的增資案主要是為支撐長期營運資金及較大資本支出。

3. Q：雷射封裝產線何時建好並貢獻營收？ GIS在此領域相對於現有供應商的優勢在
哪？  
A：產線今年 2Q26已陸續建置， 4Q26客戶原材料供應及認證趨於成熟，量產預計在 11月
或 12月從小量往上衝。 GIS的優勢在於：(1)長期與國際大廠合作累積的製造管理 能力。(2)
擁有充足現金流與財務實力，能支撐數十億的資本支出。

### Page 3

【中信投顧投資分析報告】 
個股摘要

202 6 /11 /10

3 
4. Q：雷射出貨的規格？今明兩年的資本支出預估？   
A：目前專注於大於 300mW、400mW的高功率雷射（不涉獵傳統 100mW），主要應用於
ELSFP等 200G以上高階領域，材料主力為磷化銦，同時也開發 CW Laser及 VCSEL封裝。
今年已核准資本支出約 80億元，明年資本支出將視新產品狀況及客戶需求而定，預計今年
底或明年初會較明確。

5. Q：明年筆電與平板的記憶體缺貨狀況會好轉嗎？   
A：據業界交流，因新產能開出尚需時間，記憶體缺貨狀況到 2027年底前改善幅度可能有
限，許多人預估缺貨可能持續到 2028年。

6. Q：FAU的佈局？與集團內的訊芯有無合作？   
A：集團內公司皆獨立營運，訊芯比較接近光模塊加工。 GIS初期不會聚焦整套 FAU模組
組裝，而是先從 passive component（如 Fiber holder、V型槽、透鏡陣列、超透鏡）做起，
未來若元件做得不錯，才會考慮後段的模組。

7. Q：大客戶即將推出搭載 OLED及觸控功能的筆電，貴司是否有參與？  A：不便針對
單一客戶產品發表評論。但只要是分離式的觸控模組（非內嵌式 In-cell Touch） ，公司都會
積極爭取並參與。

8. Q：雷射封裝做到 300mW以上，在技術或設備上有何門檻與優勢？  A：100mW提升
到 300mW或 400mW，封裝的製程流程基本相同，但測試項目及設備必須大幅更新，這也
是新資本支出的重點。基於過去在顯示器背光模組及指紋辨識貼合所累積的光學製程經
驗，跨入此領域對公司來說並非太難。

### Page 4

【中信投顧投資分析報告】 
個股摘要

202 6 /11 /10

4 
9. Q：設備交期似乎很長，何時能滿產？  
A：固晶機、打線機是泛用的，依照現有機台去挪動不難，主要是測試設備（如測試設備
外掛的電流計、頻譜分析儀）交期確實較長，預計 2Q27季底到 3Q27初產能可完全開出。
2Q27量產時預計會有一到兩家客戶。

10. Q：滿產能時的營收貢獻預估？何時能回收資本支出？   
A：雷射封裝商業模式以加工費為主，營收絕對金額佔比看似較小，但扣除材料費後，純
加工毛利率遠高於傳統觸控顯示業務。董事會對資本支出有嚴格的財務紀律，原則上必須
在少數幾年內完成回收。

11. Q：到 2H27可能 300mW以上的雷射市場變成熟，公司屆時產 能到位可以拿下多少市
佔? 
A：公司有接觸多位 high power 的客戶，每個客戶需求都很可觀，即便現在因為選舉政治
議題等潛在變數可能改變 AI的需求，但以整個產業來看，公司建置的產能僅占需求面的
一部分比例，仍有很大的增加產能的空間。

12. Q：與富采的 Micro LED及光通訊發展有何競爭或關聯？   
A：富采沒有做到封裝， GIS做封裝，雙方可能為上下游關係。富采 Micro LED偏向 TV巨
量轉移應用， GIS則聚焦 AR投影機光機應用，兩者 Micro LED大小差異很大。公司也與
客戶在探討 Micro LED或 Micro VCSEL 在光通訊領域的潛力，均處於樣品開發階段。另外
公司今年 4Q26或 1Q27 AR projector 可能會有小量出貨。

13. Q：光通訊相關的研發專案進度？以及信賴性測試（如 5000小時）的狀況？ FAU會朝
模組發展嗎？   
A：不便針對單一客戶及具體的信賴性測試時數評論。關於 FAU，初期聚焦於光學元件（如
Fiber holder、波導、 V型槽、超透鏡） 。公司已具備Metalens自行設計及製造的能力，因
其製程與目前開發的 AR眼鏡波導製程十分相近。

Editorial 
Opportunities from the bond sell-off  
Strategy summary: The global bond sell-off is creating tactical 
and medium-term opportunities. In the US, September’s yield 
surge has improved the risk -reward in 10 -year government 
bonds, presenting an attractive entry point for investors with a 
6-12-month horizon. We believe bond and money markets are 
overly hawkish about the Fed outlook, given disinflation is likely 
to return as impact of oil prices and tariffs ease next year. 
In equities, we stick to quality, especially sectors riding the AI -
led earnings boom. The upcoming Q3 earnings season is likely 
to show AI -investments are powering sectors beyond the 
technology and communications services, such as financials, 
materials and power and electrification. We remain constructive 
on the semiconductor and memory segments. We also expect 
the US Dollar to peak in the next few weeks along with short -
to-medium-term US bond yields. This creates opportunities for 
AUD corporate bonds, Emerging Market USD bonds and gold. 
Attractive risk -reward in US 10 -year government bonds.  
The US 10-year bond yield rose more than 50bps in September 
(biggest monthly rise since 2022) and 115bps this year, amid 
expectations of economic resilience, higher Fed rates, fuel -
driven inflation, AI-related capital demand and fiscal concerns. 
Valuations appear close to fair value, improving the 6-12-month 
risk-reward. Bond arithmetic suggests losses of less than 1% if 
the yield rises to 6%, vs. more than 10% gains if it falls to 4.5%. 
Resilient US economic activity, but softer -than-expected 
inflation. September data showed robust US employment 
(jobless claims, ADP) and core capital-goods orders. However, 
consumer confidence remains depressed, real disposable 
income is flat and households are drawing on savings to sustain 
spending. Against this divided backdrop, core PCE inflation - 
the Fed’s preferred gauge - was softer than expected at 0.2% 
m/m and 3.0% y/y. A revised method to measure PCE inflation 
also indicate that annual core PCE inflation was 35bps lower 
than previously reported, narrowing the gap with CPI inflation. 
Markets appear overly hawkish on the Fed. Money markets 
price about 85bps of tightening over the next 12 months. A 
strong labour market (September’s non -farm payrolls data is 
due tonight) may justify one or two further hikes, but aggressive 
tightening after the sharp rise in bond yields – which has 
already tightened financial conditions - could threaten financial 
stability. US small- and mid-cap equities have already fallen 8–
9% from recent peaks over the past couple of months, 
materially underperforming large caps, as energy and 
borrowing-cost concerns intensify. W hile some Fed speakers 
remain hawkish, we  agree with New York Fed President 
Williams’ that there is no urgency to tighten. We see little 
chance of an October Fed rate hike and expect inflation data 
for Sep-Nov determining whether the Fed moves in December. 
Stay with quality equities and AI beneficiaries.  US large 
caps have absorbed the rise in bond yields relatively well, 
supported by expectations of robust earnings growth led by AI-
related investment. We continue to favour quality companies 
and sectors benefiting from this AI cycle. Semiconductors and 
memory remain preferred exposures: structural AI demand 
should extend the memory upcycle, while long -term supply 
agreements provide better revenue visibility. The opportunity is 
also broadening beyond technology and communication 
services into financials, materials, power and electrification. 
Opportunities in AUD corporate bonds and gold.  The USD 
rebound alongside higher US yields offers a tactical opportunity 
to add higher -yielding Emerging Market USD and non -USD 
bonds. We remain positive on AUD corporate bonds, which 
offer a yield premium over comparable US credit. Our 
constructive medium-term AUD/USD view could add returns for 
USD-based investors over the next 6 –12 months. We would 
also use gold’s recent pullback to add exposure, supported by 
sustained Emerging Market central bank demand. We continue 
to view USD 4,000/oz as important technical support for gold.  
—  Rajat Bhattacharya

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 2 October 2026

Top client questions 
  Do you expect more RBA rate hikes after its less hawkish 
guidance? What is your view on the AUD and AUD bonds? 
Our view: We expect the RBA to leave rates unchanged, but 
retain the option to hike again should inflation intensify. We 
maintain our medium-term constructive AUD view and maintain 
our opportunistically bullish AUD corporate bonds idea. 
Rationale: The RBA’s forward guidance remains data dependent, 
explicitly allowing further tightening if needed as inflation remains too 
high.  At 4.60%, Australia’s cash rate is currently 60–85bp above the 
Fed’s 3.75–4.00% range. Thus, even with our two additional 25bp s 
Fed hikes expectation, Australian rates would hold a modest positive 
rate differential. We expect the AUD to strengthen once the Fed 
signals that US rates are near their peak, which should allow resilient 
Australian fundamentals to reassert themselves. Key risks are 
persistently high US yields, weaker China growth or a sharper 
deterioration in global risk sentiment. 
We believe risk-reward has become more attractive for AUD bonds 
following the bear -flattening of the AUD yield curve. Markets are 
already pricing in one more RBA hike through end-2027, presenting 
an attractive opportunity to earn a yield even as increasingly 
restrictive financial conditions reinforc e disinflation over time. 
Meanwhile, the positive interest rate differential with the US supports 
the currency outlook, supporting the case for AUD bonds. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist 
— Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  What does the incoming US data tell us about the path for 
US 10-year bond yields from here? 
Our view: The risk -reward in US 10 -year yields has turned 
attractive following their move higher amid incoming US data. 
Rationale: Recent US data has indeed started to show softer-than-
expected inflation. However, market technicals are likely temporarily 
delaying a move lower in bond yields, with reports suggesting the  
rise in yields is creating forced selling amongst hedge funds and real 
estate investment trusts. Nevertheless, we do not expect this to be 
an extended selloff. We have initiated an opportunistic bullish idea 
on US 10-year government bonds this week and continue to believe 
this idea offers an attractive risk/reward on a 6-12-month horizon as 
yields become attractive enough to entice buyer flow to return. On a 
broader allocation basis, we retain our structural preference for 3-7-
year maturities in bonds. 
— Cedric Lam, Senior Investment Strategist

Australia-US interest rate differentials and positive

### Page 5

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 2 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  How should investors position in the GBP ahead of the UK 
budget announcement next month? 
Our view: We maintain a medium-term constructive GBP view, 
but keep near-term exposure measured ahead of the 28 October 
Budget. We would view any pre -Budget weakness as an 
opportunity to gradually build GBP exposure. 
Rationale: The government’s decision to adjust the pension triple 
lock from 2030 is an early indication of greater willingness to tackle 
longer-term spending pressures, reinforcing Chancellor Healey’s 
commitment to fiscal discipline and meeting the fiscal rules. Markets 
are likely to scrutinise the Budget closely for evidence that this 
discipline extends to near -term tax and spending decisions. A 
credible Budget should help contain pressure on UK government 
bond yields and support GBP. Any fiscal slippage remains a key risk.  
Near term, we prefer measured GBP exposure as elevated US yields 
and repricing towards further Fed tightening could continue to 
support the USD. Over the medium term, we expect GBP to rebound 
as the Fed eventually signals the peak of its tightening cycle. 
—  Vincent Tan, Senior Investment Strategist 
—  Iris Yuen, Investment Strategist 
  How would different US mid-term election outcome 
scenarios affect the outlook for US equities? 
Our view: We believe election outcomes will have a limited 
impact over the next 12 months. We remain positive on US 
equities driven by fundamental earnings growth.  
Rationale: Over the past 50 years, the ruling US President’s party 
has retained control of both chambers of Congress on only two 
occasions (1978 and 2002). All other elections result ed in an 
opposition sweep or a divided government.  
Based on the latest prediction markets, it appears that an opposition 
sweep (both the House and Senate flips from Republican to 
Democrats) is more likely than a divided government ( in which the 
House flips to Democrats , while the Senate remains under 
Republican control). A scenario where Republicans retain a majority 
is least likely per prediction markets. 
While mid -term elections may introduce near -term volatility, we 
caution against overreaction. Historically, the average return for the 
S&P 500 following an opposition sweep is 17% over the next 12 
months, but the average return following a divided government is not 
significantly worse, at 15%. We prefer to base our investment views 
on fundamental earnings growth, where investor attention should 
focus on the upcoming Q3 earnings season. 
—  Jason Wong, Senior Equity Analyst

### Page 6

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 2 October 2026

Top client questions (cont’d) 
  What is the outlook for  the memory chip and broader 
semiconductors industry? 
Our view: We remain positive on semiconductors and memory 
chips. Structural AI demand should prolong the memory 
upcycle while long-term agreements improve revenue visibility. 
Rationale: Recent industry commentary points to tighter memory 
supply-demand conditions through 2028, reinforcing our view that AI 
demand is likely to extend the memory upcycle. Furthermore, adoption 
of long-term agreements improves revenue and earnings visibility. 
Despite debates around “Pacing the Frontier”, we have seen a flurry 
of new model releases, along with the introduction of new consumer 
AI agents capable of comparing prices online and making 
purchases. These are early days, but the path is open for a business-
to-agent (B2A) economy where businesses increasingly interact with 
AI assistants acting on customers’ behalf. As these applications are 
increasingly inference intensive, wider adoption should support 
demand for memory, with its share of global semiconductor 
revenues projected to rise from 30.7% in 2025 to 66.7% in 2027. 
Against this backdrop, we maintain our Opportunistic idea on Global 
Semiconductors. Given memory  chips’ cyclical nature, investors 
should avoid overconcentration in memory names and maintain a 
diversified exposure across Big Tech and semiconductors. 
—  Ryan Goh, Investment Strategist 
  Does a US bond yield stress test raise the risk to your 
constructive view on US equities? 
Our view: We have a target for the S&P 500 index of 8400 over 
the next 12 months. A scenario of a sustained rise in the US 10-
year government bond yield to 5.6% could lead to negative 
returns from the current S&P500 level (7,666). 
Rationale: Our base case 12-month target for the S&P 500 is 8,400, 
which assumes 2027 S&P 500 EPS of USD 420 and a 20x forward 
P/E multiple. A rise in bond yields would have an impact on equity 
valuations, as a higher discount rate reduces the present value of 
future earnings. W e stress -test our target under different 10 -year 
yield and earnings scenarios.  
A rise in bond yields would arguably be associated with a stronger 
growth environment and hence, even higher earnings. However, 
assuming unchanged EPS at our base case of USD 420, a 
(sustained) rise in the 10 -year bond yield to 5.6% would lead to 
negative returns from the current level  if not offset by a further 
improvement in earnings growth . However, in o ur base case  we 
expect the 10-year yield is more likely to fall from here. 
—  Fook Hien Yap, Senior Investment Strategist

The memory segment is projected to account for

## Document 442: US Midterms_ The cost of living is now a pivotal issue (2026-11-02)

Source PDF: all report pdf/US Midterms_ The cost of living is now a pivotal issue (2026-11-02).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
3
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.40 / 2 November 2026EDITORIAL
US MIDTERMS: THE COST OF LIVING IS NOW A PIVOTAL ISSUE
1 AP-NORC, October 2026.
2 Jared Bernstein, Daniel Posthumus, ‘The way we were: Price-level shocks and consumers’ memories’, SIEPR, May 2026.
3 72% of registered voters rank ending the war with Iran as one of the top priorities for the next Congress, including 60% of Republicans (AP-NORC, October 2026).

4 Oleg Korenok, David Munro, Jiayi Chen, ‘Inflation and attention thresholds’, GLO Discussion Paper Series, No. 1175, Global Labour Organization, 2022.
5 Rajashri Chakrabarti, Thu Pham, Beck Pierce, Maxim L. Pinkovskiy, ‘Tracking the K-shaped economy: who’s driving spending?’, Federal Reserve Bank of New York, Liberty
Street Economics, 1st May 2026.
6 The year 2020 is excluded due to the disruptions caused by lockdowns.
In the United States, growth is holding up, unemployment remains low and consumer spending shows no sign of slowing.

On paper, that should flatter the president’s party ahead of the midterms. Yet, Republicans could lose control of Congress.
The rise in the cost of living, which weighs on households far more than macroeconomic aggregates suggest, could be one
of the reasons. On top of persistent inflation, rekindled by the war in Iran, there is also a slowdown in wage growth and
rising housing costs. The election could hinge on affordability, yet whichever party wins will lack the means to address it.

A change in the majority in Congress will nonetheless have an impact on the country’s economy.
On 3 rd November, Americans will elect a new Congress for the next
two years. With Republicans currently in control of the White House,
the House of Representatives and the Senate, the balance of power in
Washington could shift. Polls suggest that the House, at least, could
swing to the Democrats. Beyond the new balance of power, the vote
will reflect how voters view the way the country is being run. Here, we

focus on the cost of living.
INFLATION: THE INCUMBENTS’ BURDEN
The incumbent party goes into the election with two handicaps.
The first is structural: no matter who holds the White House, the pre -
sident’s party almost always loses seats in midterm elections. The
second stems from the combination of robust but uneven growth
and persistent inflation, which is undermining household confidence.
Pre-election polls1 confirm this assessment: the economy and the cost-

of-living top voters’ worries.
Inflation has overshot the Fed’s target every year since 2021. Prices are
up 28% over five years: 31% for housing (not including mortgage rates,
which have more than doubled), and 29% for food. This shock is felt all
the more keenly as it is the most severe in forty years and follows on
from decades of low inflation2. Furthermore, it hits the lowest-income
households the hardest. In addition, the election comes after an ener-

gy-driven rise in inflation. Energy accounts for the entire gap between
the latest CPI reading (3.7% y/y in September) and the last figure re -
corded before the conflict in the Middle East started (2.4% y/y).
Fuel has made things worse. The rise in the price of a gallon (46%
between February and October) caused by the Iran war — which is
unpopular even amongst Republican voters3 — is an aggravating factor.
In 2026, inflation only slightly crossed the ‘attention threshold’, the

3.6%y/y rate beyond which households start to regard it as problema -
tic4. Fuel is a recurring, unavoidable expense whose price is directly
observable, unlike many items in the CPI basket. This results in a subs-
tantial gap between perceived inflation and measured inflation.
Inflation must be viewed alongside the other drivers of households’
well-being. Today, wages, payrolls and the housing market are moving
the wrong way for most Americans.
IT'S NOT JUST PRICES

The 2022 midterms suggest caution in reading the polls. Democrats
defied expectations of a ‘Republican wave’, retaining control of the Se-
nate and losing the House by just a few seats, even though inflation
averaged 8% that year. This reminds us that inflation must be viewed
alongside the other drivers of households’ well-being. Indeed, how
people experience the cost of living depends on the interplay between
prices, wages and housing. In 2022, wages, payrolls and the housing

market were all growing faster than in 2026. Today, all three are mo -
ving the wrong way for most Americans.
Wage growth is slowing just as inflation picks up again. In September
2026, average hourly earnings were up 3% y/y in nominal terms, the
slowest rate since 2018, and now below inflation. Real wage earnings
are therefore falling (-0.7% y/y in September, with six out of nine mon-
ths showing a decline in 2026). The rise in the cost of living is thus

compounded by a net loss of purchasing power. The Atlanta Fed reports
that wage growth is lower for the bottom quartile of earners (the 25%
of employees with the lowest pay) than for the top quartile (the 25%
with the highest pay): 3.8% y/y vs. 4.4% on average since late 2024,
following 10 years in which the gap favoured the bottom quartile. Top
earners also benefit most from the wealth effect linked to stock mar -
ket valuations. This explains the resilience of aggregate consumption.

However, this rests on a relatively narrow base, mostly households
earning more than USD 125,000/year5. Meanwhile, a falling saving rate
(3.8% in September 2026, down 1.4pp y/y) suggests households are
drawing on their savings to offset price rises. A large share of the po -
pulation is therefore excluded from the growth that has been observed.
Beyond wages, there is the question of employment prospects.
The unemployment rate remains low and consistent with full employ-

ment. Yet the labour market looks stagnant: low firing despite repeated
shocks and low hiring despite solid growth. Payroll growth (an average
of 68,000 per month since January) is well below that of the previous
decade (nearly 200,000 per month between 2011 and 2019 6). Com-
bined with a workforce that is no longer growing due to an ageing
population and a restrictive immigration policy, this is enough to keep
the unemployment rate low and the labour market looking healthy.

### Page 2

4
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.40 / 2 November 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
But households are likely to be suffering from a lack of opportunities,
and consequently from a lack of prospects for wage growth that would
help them to increase their purchasing power. The Conference Board’s
labour differential, a gauge of perceived job availability, says as much:
outside the pandemic, it is at its lowest since 2015.
Housing is the area that has deteriorated the most. The rise in

mortgage rates that began in 2022, from under 4% to over 7%, has
dampened the property market. This is true on the demand side, as
expected, but also on the supply side. The sharp rise in interest rates
is deterring homeowners with low-interest mortgages from selling, as
they would have to borrow again at current rates. This ‘lock-in effect’
prevents prices from falling despite weaker demand. The lock-in effect
is also affecting tenants, as the decline in the availability of housing is

driving up rents. Housing lies at the heart of the disconnect between
the macroeconomic picture and voters’ perception of it, as they face a
multifaceted rise in the cost of living. That rise is also driven by credit:
monthly repayments on a home of the same value have risen shar -
ply. This additional cost, which inflation figures do not capture7, erodes
households’ purchasing power in the property market.
A CONGRESS POWERLESS ON THE COST OF LIVING

The next Congress will, in all likelihood, have little room for ma -
noeuvre. Neither party is within reach of the thresholds required to
override the filibuster (which allows 41 out of 100 senators to block
a bill from coming to a vote) and the presidential veto (two-thirds of
both chambers).
Affordability is likely to be a key issue in the election, but the winners
will lack the tools to address it. The Democrats’ agenda relies notably
on the reintroduction of schemes that were abolished or scaled back

under the current administration (Affordable Care Act tax credits, Me-
dicaid). Even if Congress passed them, they would be subject to a pre-
sidential veto. There is no question of direct support for households or
a restrictive fiscal policy.
Whether inflation falls will depend on two factors: how the conflict in
Iran unfolds and the effectiveness of monetary policy. The labour mar-
ket should hold its current balance: robust growth (see our EcoFlash)

is supporting demand, whilst failing to alleviate supply-side pressures,
and the unemployment rate could continue to fall. Household senti -
ment will continue to depend on how prices develop. As for housing,
it is unlikely to improve in the short term. With the 10-year Treasury
yield still at 4.8% at the end of 2027 (BNP Paribas forecast), 30-year
mortgage rates would, at best, remain around 7%.
THE OTHER ECONOMIC STAKES OF THE VOTE
Historically, divided governments have brought legislative stability,

which is viewed favourably by the markets. But polarisation has in -
creased in recent years and is likely to feature prominently in the bud-
get vote and the debt-ceiling debate. The debt limit, in particular, could
be reached as early as Q1 2027, depending on the Treasury’s borrowing
requirements. The current administration, meanwhile, has taken major
decisions (on tariffs, immigration and Iran) without involving Congress,
a practice that is likely to continue.

7 Marijn A. Bolhuis, Judd N. L. Cramer, Karl Oskar Schulz, Lawrence H. Summers, ‘The cost of money is part of the cost of living: new evidence on the consumer sentiment
anomaly’, NBER Working Paper, No. 32163, 2024.
Risks to the deficit remain tilted to the upside. According to our base -
line scenario, it is expected to remain stable at 6.3% of GDP in 2027,
but the post-election balance of power could add to the cost of the war
in Iran and rising debt-service costs. In particular, interests could align

in favour of increased spending should the Democrats gain control of
both houses of Congress. Avoiding, or ending, a shutdown could require
a trade-off involving mutual concessions from the presidency on key,
popular aspects of the Democratic agenda (such as healthcare), and
from the Democrats on defence.
The AI sector is exposed to the vote. The economic stakes are high
given how much the associated investment cycle and the wealth effect
contribute to US growth. However, at this stage, the risk is not legisla-

tive. As with the cost of living, sweeping legislation is unlikely, all the
more so with a president holding a pro-AI stance, and who holds the
right of veto. The risk lies rather in sentiment and further out. A Demo-
cratic sweep could lead to increased congressional activism on regu -
lation, on concentration in the AI industry, and on the knock-on effects
on the cost of living (data centres and electricity) and on jobs. Putting
these issues on the agenda could create a climate conducive to tighter

regulation in the medium term and weigh on the sector’s valuations.
Furthermore, the mid-term elections will see 36 governors’ seats up
for election. These governors have extensive powers (regarding autho-
risations and incentives) when it comes to the establishment of data
centres in their states.
Finally, the midterms will matter for monetary policy. The Senate
confirms, by simple majority, the president’s nominees to the Fed’s
Board of Governors. Consequently, should the Senate come under De -

mocratic control, President Trump would need a consensus pick to re-
place Governor Powell from 2028 — or to fill any other seat that might
become vacant during the next Congress.
*****
Whatever the outcome of the election, disinflation and, more broadly,
relief from the cost of living, depend less on Congress than on the
White House and, to a lesser extent, the Fed. The key factor lies in the
resolution of the conflict in Iran, coupled with an effective monetary

policy to enable a sustainable return to price stability.
Anis Bensaidani
anis.bensaidani@bnpparibas.com

## Document 444: USA_ Pushing Back Fed Rate Hike on Lower Core PCE Inflation; Q2 GDP Revised Up to +2.2%; Trade Deficit Widens More Than Expected; Lowering Q3 GDP Tracking to +3.3%

Source PDF: all report pdf/USA_ Pushing Back Fed Rate Hike on Lower Core PCE Inflation; Q2 GDP Revised Up to +2.2%; Trade Deficit Widens More Than Expected; Lowering Q3 GDP Tracking to +3.3%.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs Publishing
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 2

Personal consumption (third estimate) +3.8% (qoq ar) for Q2 vs. GS +3.4%, median
forecast +3.4%, prior revised +0.7%
Personal income +0.2% for August vs. GS +0.5%, median forecast +0.5%, prior revised
+0.3%
Personal spending +0.9% for August vs. GS +0.8%, median forecast +0.9%, prior revised
+0.1%
Advance goods trade balance -$132.6bn for August vs. GS -$116.0bn, median forecast

+36k
MAIN POINTS:
1. The core PCE index increased +0.25% in August, somewhat below expectations, and
the year-over-year rate increased 3.01%. We estimate that the Dallas Fed’s trimmed
mean PCE measure increased 0.16% month over month and 2.20% year over year.
2. Year-over-year core PCE was revised downward 0.36pp in July to 2.98%, primarily
reflecting the methodological revisions to the calculation of the portfolio management
(-0.23pp), computer accessories and software (-0.11pp), and legal services (+0.01pp)

components of PCE. These downward revisions were larger than we expected, mostly
reflecting a larger revision to the portfolio management component than we expected.
3. Today’s inflation report, especially the downward revisions resulting from the
methodological changes, imply that core PCE inflation is likely to substantially
undershoot the median FOMC participant’s forecast of 3.4% for 2026 on a Q4/Q4 basis.
We now expect a 3.0% increase for core PCE in 2026 on a Q4/Q4 basis. Coupled with

comments from New York Fed President John Williams yesterday that “with the policy
action we took at our September meeting, there is no need for urgency, and we have
time to gather more information,” we now think that an October hike is unlikely.  We are
pushing back the second hike in our forecast to December, and we see a strong chance
that the FOMC will ultimately conclude that additional rate hikes are unnecessary.
4. Real GDP was revised up by 0.7pp to +2.2% in Q2 (quarter-over-quarter annualized),

largely reflecting upward revisions to consumption (+0.4pp to +3.8%) and investment
(+1.9pp to +4.6%) growth. The strength in consumer spending in Q2 likely continued to
reflect the boost to consumer cashflow from larger-than-usual tax refunds, but we
continue to expect consumer spending growth to slow somewhat in 2026Q4 as the fiscal
support fades and the headwind from higher energy prices remains. Stronger
investment growth reflected an upward revision to private fixed investment (+0.5pp to

+9.0%), driven by data center investment in the nonresidential structures category
(+1.9pp to +0.1%), and an upward revision to the contribution from inventories (+0.2pp
to -0.5pp).
5. Reflecting today’s annual revisions to the national accounts, real GDP was revised up
by 0.4pp to +2.5% in Q1 (quarter-over-quarter annualized), largely reflecting upward
revisions to the contributions from consumption and exports. Revisions for prior years
were modest: real GDP growth on a Q4/Q4 basis was unrevised for 2025 (+2.0%) and

2023 (+3.4%), and slightly revised down for 2024 (-0.2pp to +2.4%) and up for 2022
30 September 2026   2
Goldman Sachs

### Page 3

(+0.1pp to +1.3%). 
6. Personal income increased 0.2% in August, below expectations. Year-over-year 
disposable personal income growth reached 4.8% in August. Personal spending rose 
0.9% in August, in line with consensus expectations. The savings rate decreased 0.5pp to 
4.1% in August, after a large 1.6pp upward revision to the savings rate last month driven 
by an upward revision to the level of disposable personal income (+1.2pp contribution) 
and a downward revision to personal spending as a share of DPI (+0.4pp). 
7. According to the Advance Economic Indicators Report, the goods trade deficit 
widened by $13.7bn to $132.6bn (seasonally adjusted) in August, well above 
expectations. Goods exports increased by $3.7bn to $203.4bn, largely driven by an 
increase in industrial supplies exports. Goods imports increased by $17.4bn to 
$336.1bn, driven by a large increase in industrial supplies and capital goods imports. 
8. According to the ADP report, private sector employment rose by 90k in September 
(month-over-month, seasonally adjusted), slightly above consensus expectations. 
Employment in the services sector rose 59k, primarily reflecting a 55k increase in 
education and health services and a 22k increase in leisure and hospitality employment 
that were partly offset by a 16k decrease in financial activities employment. 
Employment in goods-producing industries increased by 31k, driven by a 17k increase in 
manufacturing and a 15k increase in construction employment. ADP’s new median 
annual base pay measure, which better tracks other measures of wage growth than its 
gross pay measure, increased 3.0% for job stayers, unchanged from the August pace, 
and 4.8% for job changers, 0.1pp above the August pace. We left our forecast for August 
nonfarm payroll growth unchanged at +80k (vs. +90k consensus) ahead of Friday’s 
release. 
9. The details of the personal income and spending report were firmer than our previous 
GDP tracking assumptions. While the trade-related details of the Advance Economic 
Indicators report were much weaker than our previous assumptions, suggesting notably 
stronger imports growth in Q3 than we previously assumed, the inventories details were 
significantly stronger. On net, we lowered our Q3 GDP tracking estimate by 0.1pp to 
+3.3% (quarter-over-quarter annualized).

## Document 462: 『主な意見』に「主な異見」なし

Source PDF: all report pdf/『主な意見』に「主な異見」なし.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 11 月 10 日(火)

1/3 
『主な意見』に「主な異見」なし    
 経済調査部主席エコノミスト藤代宏一 
（℡：050-5474-6123）

・10月金融政策決定会合における『主な意見』が公表された。注目は、先行きの利上げ見通し
もさることながら、浅田統一郎委員と佐藤綾野委員の物価と金融政策運営に関する認識であ
る。政策金利を1.25％に引き上げた９月会合において浅田委員と佐藤委員は双方とも反対票
を投じていた。浅田委員は「足もとの消費者物価（除く生鮮食品）の上昇率が２％を下回る
なか、経済の状況も必ずしも強いとはいえない点を踏まえると、金融市場調節方針を据え置
くことが望ましい」、佐藤委員は「経済・物価情勢が、現時点ではこれまでと比べて大きく
加速している状態にあるわけではないと考えられるもとで、このタイミングでの利上げは適
切ではない」と述べていた。こうした認識に変化がないなら、基調的な物価上昇率が２％に
達したとの認識に異論を唱えるのが自然であると考えられるが、10月会合で示された展望レ
ポートの内容は全員一致であったことが植田総裁の記者会見で明らかになっている。

・これらを踏まえたうえで、『主な意見』における物価や金融政策運営に関する記述をみる
と、浅田委員と佐藤委員のものと思われるものがあったが、物価については下振れリスクを
殊更に強調するものはなく、慎重な声としては下記の４つがあった。浅田委員と佐藤委員の
他にも２つ、物価の下振れ方向のリスクに対する言及（と読める）記述があった点はやや意
外であった。 
・日経平均株価は先行き12 ヶ月72,000 円程度で推移するだろう 
・USD/JPY は先行き12 ヶ月 155 円程度で推移するだろう 
・日銀は政策金利を12 月に 1.5％とした後、27 年末までに2.25％とするだろう 
・FED はFF 金利を、年内は4.0％で据え置くだろう

### Page 2

2/3 
「来年の賃上げ率が本年と比べて大きく上昇する可能性は低く、現在の物価上昇の賃金への二
次的波及のリスクは高くない。むしろ、政策要因で低下している消費者物価が、今後の賃金
交渉に与える影響を確認していく必要がある」

「足もとのインフレ率は既往の原油高を背景に上昇しているが、現時点では二次的な波及効果
は限定的であると考えている。今後、賃金の動向なども踏まえながら、基調的な物価上昇率
が『物価安定の目標』と整合的な水準で定着していくかどうかを慎重に確認する必要があ
る」

「直近の消費者物価の前年比が２％台に入る中、基調的な物価上昇率が今後２％の目標水準を
超えて上昇するリスクがあると認識しているが、一方で、既往の利上げがラグを伴って今後
経済・物価を下振れさせるリスクにも留意する必要がある」

「基調的な物価上昇率がゼロ％近傍から上昇してきたわが国では、２％へのアンカー度合いが
欧米対比弱いため、外生的なショックに対する反応が大きくなる懸念がある」

・他方、金融政策運営については浅田委員と佐藤委員のものと思われる記述があり、何れもハ
ト派であった。ただし、次回以降の利上げに「必ず」反対票を投じるというほどの強い語気
は感じられない。

「引き続き政策金利を引き上げる前提のもと、慌てて中立金利の水準に引き上げなくても、基
調的な物価上昇率は先行きも概ね２％程度に定着するものと考えられる。ヘッドラインの物
価が一時的に乖離したとしても、２％に収束するメカニズムが働くことについて、対外的に
説明していくことが必要である」

「金融環境はまだ緩和的であるが、緩和の度合いは徐々に縮小している。政策金利の水準も中
立金利の推計レンジに入っており、今後の利上げが経済、ひいては基調的な物価上昇率を押
し下げるリスクに十分留意する必要がある」

・３～４ヶ月に１度の利上げが「慌てて」に該当するかは微妙であるが、次回12月会合で利上
げの提案があった場合、この（一つ目の）記述の主が反対票を投じても不思議ではない。そ

### Page 3

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
3/3
れでも、政策委員会全体としては「基調的な物価上昇率が２％の『物価安定の目標』と概ね
整合的な水準となる中、現在の金融環境が緩和的であることを踏まえると、経済・物価・金
融情勢に応じて、引き続き政策金利を引き上げ、金融緩和の度合いを調整していくことが適
当である」との考え方で一致している。筆者は引き続き、12月会合における利上げを予想す
る。もっとも、世界的にＡＩ・半導体株の調整が長引くようだと、2027年１月まで待つ選択
肢も浮上するとみている。

市場對法國財政可持續性以及其可能產生的外溢擔

預估歐股短線將延續震盪走勢，維持中立評等。

資料來源：野村，2026/10/7，中國信託商業銀行彙整，2026/10。
2027年預算提案樽節開支，但將面對政治角力
《法國財政疑慮、政治僵局與社會動盪導致法德利
差擴大，明年大選前政治風險溢酬預估維持高檔》
十月份法國公債殖利率大幅上升 ，反映法國政治
局勢分裂 、財政整頓前景不明 ，加上明年總統大
選恐由極右派勝出 ，市場對法國財政可持續性的
疑慮升高 ，法國十年期公債殖利率 10月下旬一度
突破5%，創2002年7月以來新高。

近期法國各地爆發大規模抗議活動 ，亦對市場帶
來衝擊 。法國政治與財政風險升溫 ，反而吸引資
金轉向德國公債避險 ，加上近期金融條件趨緊 、
市場對歐央升息的預期相較九月底降溫 ，推動德
國十年期公債殖利率自高檔回落 ，法德利差大幅
擴大 、10/22一度突破 170個基點 ，創2011年11
月歐債危機以來新高(統計至10/26)。
針對2027年預算 ，法國政府提出 430億歐元財政
整頓方案，目標將財政赤字占 GDP比率自2026年
的5.4%降至2027年的5%，措施包括凍結公部門
薪資、限制每月超過1,260歐元的退休金通膨調整
幅度、延長針對大型企業的特別稅但縮小適用範
圍等。反對黨已對多項措施表達反對 ，若不採取
任何樽節措施 ，2027年法國財政赤字占 GDP比率
預估將達6.5%。由於大選在即 ，從近期極右派國
民聯盟釋出的意向來看 ，雖不支持預算案 ，卻也
不願在大選前引發政治危機 ，預估現任總理勒克
努仍有機會透過援引 《憲法》第49.3條於年底前
通過一份財政整頓力度較有限的預算案 。然而，

即使預算案過關，也難扭轉法國債務攀升趨勢。

重點摘要
3
近來融資成本 上升進一步加重法國的債務負擔 。
信評機構 惠譽(2026/10/12)上調利息支出預測 ，
預估2026年至2028年間，利息支出將累積佔GDP
的0.8%。利息支出占GDP比率預估將由2026年約
2.5%升至 20 28年約3.3%，2030年進一步升至
3.8%，反映高融資成本對財政的壓力將逐步顯現 。
利息支出增加將壓縮其他公共支出及財政空間 。
若赤字改善幅度不如預期 ，債務比率恐持續上升 。
2027年總統大選亦將牽動法國財政政策與債市走
向。法國總統大選預計於 2027年4至5月舉行，第
一輪投票訂於 4/18，若無候選人取得過半選票將
於5/2進行第二輪投票。最新民調顯示，極右派國
民聯盟候選人勒龐在首輪支持度領先 ，超過三成；

極左派法國不屈黨候選人梅朗雄近期支持度上升 ，
引發市場擔憂將發生沒有中間派候選人進入第二
輪決選的不利情境。
梅朗雄主張擴張性財政政策 ，對市場最為不利 ，
勝選機率較高的勒龐 10/6提出財政整頓方案並且
主張建立一個可信的債務穩定框架 ，試圖安撫市
場對法國財政前景的疑慮 。在面對市場壓力下 ，
勒龐表達更具財政紀律的立場有助稍稍緩和財政
擴張的擔憂 ，但其財政整頓方案的政策假設與具
體實施存在不確定性 ，加上大選後很可能緊接著
進行國會改選 ，政治版圖維持分裂狀態不利財政
改革。
資料來源：Fitch，2026/10/12，中國信託商業銀行彙整，2026/10。

重點摘要
4
整體而言 ，財政和政治不確定性預估將令法德利
差維持在高位 ，下檔情境為若 2027年預算案無法
於年底前通過 ，恐將面臨更大的財政惡化與市場
壓力，十年期法德利差恐有進一步擴大的風險。
《目前情勢屬法國財政信心危機，而非歐洲主權風
險全面升溫》
儘管投資人對法國債券要求更高的風險補償 ，但
近期法國公債標售仍有足夠的認購需求 。除此之
外，目前法德利差擴大對整體歐洲市場的外溢影
響尚屬可控 ，多數歐洲國家相對德國利差雖受牽
動近期亦出現較明顯的擴大 ，但利差水準仍可控 。
以政府債務占 GDP比重較高的義大利來看 ，十年
期義德利差 10/22一度升至 124個基點 ，回升至
2025年4月水準，但仍遠低於 2011年歐債危機時

期。因此，目前情勢仍偏向法國自身的財政信心
危機，而非歐洲主權信用風險全面升溫。
資料來源：Bloomberg，2024年來至2026/10/21，中國信託商業銀行彙整，2026/10。

5
• 10月歐元區 PMI維持擴張但

《歐元區PMI維持擴張但成長放緩，通膨壓力面臨
上行風險》
歐元區10月綜合PMI初值由9月的53.1降至51.9，
雖連續四個月高於 50、顯示經濟持續擴張 ，但成
長動能有所放緩 。法國受到大規模罷工與抗議活
動影響 ，商業活動下滑 ，成為主要拖累因素之一 ，
法國10月綜合PMI初值降至48.4，明顯落後其他
歐元區國家 。另一方面 ，能源及投入成本 上升迫
使企業提高售價，通膨壓力未明顯緩解。
中東緊張局勢延宕，布蘭特原油期貨價格升至116

美元/桶，TTF天然氣期貨價格升至96歐元/兆瓦時，
天然氣價格持續突破今年高點(統計至10/26)。
目前能源價格高於歐洲央行九月的基準與不利情
境預測 ，歐元區通膨面臨上行風險 ，不過未出現
明顯的二輪通膨效應 ，經濟成長則面臨下行壓力 ，
預期歐洲央行連續升息機率低 。此外，近期公債
殖利率上升亦發揮了緊縮效果 ，預估歐洲央行將
暫時按兵不動 。待12月份利率會議 ，屆時歐洲央
行將取得更新的經濟預測 ，可據此重新評估通膨
與成長前景 ，若能源價格及通膨壓力持續偏高 ，
不排除12月將再升息一碼 ，將存款機制利率調升
至2.75%。
針對近期主權債券殖利率大幅走升 ，預估歐洲央
行將密切關注其走勢 ，但長債利率的上升尚未達
到表明主權債券市場面臨顯著壓力的水平 ，應不
足以觸發政策調整。
資料來源：摩根士丹利，2026/10/22，中國信託商業銀行彙整，2026/10。

重點摘要
7
《融資成本上升對歐洲企業帶來壓力，然整體槓桿
率尚可控，財務體質脆弱的企業面臨較大風險》
主權債券市場的壓力令歐洲企業信用利差同步擴大，
自9月以來，歐洲投資等級債信用利差擴大31個基
點至108個基點，非投資等級債信用利差擴大幅度
較為顯著，擴大105個基點至363個基點，兩者均
回升至2025年5月以來高點(統計至10/26止)。所幸，
歐洲企業資產負債表整體仍維持穩健。儘管淨債務
/EBITDA比率自低檔回升，但整體槓桿水準與歷史
水準相比並不高，仍處健康水準。因此，債務成本
上升雖增加企業的財務負擔，尤其是財務體質較脆
弱的企業面臨較高的風險，但目前尚不至於構成系
統性威脅。另一方面，融資成本上升，企業獲利可

能面臨壓力 ，根據高盛證券 (10/23)預估，在其他
條件不變下，債務成本每上升100個基點，道瓊歐
洲600企業每股盈餘 (EPS)將減少約3%，其中財務
體質較脆弱的企業所受衝擊最大，具備強健資產負
債表與穩定利潤率的企業較能抵禦高利率影響。
《利率維持高位壓抑估值，歐股短線將延續震盪》
隨著預算協商進行、財政擔憂短線難消除，歐央12
月利率會議可能再升息一碼，公債殖利率預估將維
持高位，加上中東地緣風險未除，近期市場對美國
期中選舉後，美伊敵對行動可能重燃的預期升溫，
均將對市場情緒與歐股評價帶來壓力，預估歐股短
線將延續震盪走勢，所幸，LSEG I/B/E/S(10/22)統
計，道瓊歐洲600企業3Q26財報公布雖仍在初期階
段，但目前整體獲利優於財報季前預測，2026年企
業獲利預估相較10月初呈現上修趨勢，歐洲企業獲
利成長應有助提供下檔支撐，歐股維持中立評等。
資料來源：高盛證券，2026/10/23，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10。

8
《土耳其基金清算風波 、區域雜音增多 ，新歐展
望中立》
MSCI新興歐洲指數 10月以來下跌 5%，希臘、土
耳其與波蘭股市全數下跌 ，又以希臘與土耳其股

由金融股領跌 ，除了因今年來漲勢不錯引發獲利
了結外 ，法德利差擴大連帶推升希臘與德國公債
殖利率利差 ，市場擔憂法國債市風險蔓延 ，壓抑
銀行股回檔。土耳其股市則受到9月中旬多檔基金
爆發贖回危機所拖累 ，多家土耳其資產管理公司
未能滿足投資人贖回請求 ，土耳其資本市場委員
會下令清算七家資產管理公司管理的 131檔基金 ，
合計資產管理規模約 1兆里拉，MSCI 10/15宣布

凍結土耳其股票的部分指數調整措施 ，並警告若
土耳其未能改善市場誠信與透明度問題 ，最快將
於2027年6月考慮將其從新興市場降級 。考量地
緣風險未除 、土耳其基金清算問題造成的市場動
盪，維持新歐股市中立看法。
貨幣政策方面，9月波蘭CPI年增率升至4.1%，較
8月的3.4%上升且超過央行容忍區間上限 ，波蘭
央行10月利率會議維持政策利率於 3.75%不變。
中東地緣僵局持續 、能源價格維持高檔更久令通
膨前景存在上行風險 ，預估波蘭央行將更長時間
維持利率不變 。土耳其方面 ，10/22土耳其央行
宣布將一週附買回利率調降 100個基點 ，從37%
下調至36%，為時隔9個月再次降息，符合市場預
期，央行會後聲明顯示 ，儘管地緣政治局勢發展
令能源價格維持高檔可能對通膨前景帶來上行風
險，然而，土耳其國內需求疲軟 ，供給面衝擊對
國內價格的傳導效應有限 ，暗示有持續放鬆貨幣
政策限制性的空間。
土耳其基金九月份資產縮水390億美元

《高利高通膨壓抑，留意日股短線震盪加大》
10月日本股市大幅震盪修正 ，符合本行下調評等之
預期。其中，日經225指數在10月初一度上破7萬點
大館，此後因AI相關估值疑慮、科技股修正與長端利
率上行，全球科技股估值大幅修正，日經225指數在
10月23日盤中一度下探61,462點。
日本兩大指數在本月表現也呈現相對分化。日經225
權重權重集中於東京威力科創、愛德萬測試、軟銀集
團等半導體與 AI標的，在本波修正幅度較大；相反
的，TOPIX指數因金融、內需與防禦類股權重較高，
加上利率下行有利銀行以外的內需類股，回檔相對溫
和。也間接反映了10月的修正偏向是 「AI交易擁擠
的釋放」，而非全面性的景氣衰退定價。
債市方面，10年期日本公債殖利率在10月初一度升

至3.11%、創1999年以來新高 ，反映日本央行升
息、以及高市內閣財政擴張的雙重疑慮。
此後殖利率一路回落至 10月26日的2.82%、下行約
23個基點。這一下行幅度反映全球 AI相關信用風險
引發的避險需求。
重點摘要
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/9，中國信託商業銀行整理
5
AI相關估值疑慮、科技股修正
• 10月日股受AI估值修正及長天
期利率波動影響而震盪下跌，
但修正主要集中於半導體與AI
權值股。
• TOPIX相對抗跌，顯示市場仍
未全面反映景氣衰退，而是資
金自擁擠交易部位撤出。

《日本出口維持韌性，內需動能出現疑慮》
經濟基本面部分，日本9月出口在半導體與AI相關需
求帶動下，連續13個月維持正成長 ，惟增速放緩至
16.7%、略低於預期；但進口年增卻升至18.9%，主
要因為進口能源價格大漲所致，導致貿易逆差擴大，
外需雖強卻未能轉化為淨出口貢獻。
內需與生產端則收高利高通膨壓力同步降溫。工業生
產增速自年中高點回落，製造業PMI降至七個月來最
低，整體擴張速度已低於上半年水準，細項中新訂單
與出口訂單成長同步放緩，能源與勞動成本則大幅走
升，對企業營運形成壓力。內需改善亦相當有限，零
售銷售下滑、失業率微升、求人求職比回落，反映勞
動市場略有降溫；所幸，實質薪資已連續八個月正成
長、消費者信心小幅回升。

整體而言，日本經濟仍處於「名目成長、實質停滯」
的格局，擴張主要仰賴出口，內需復甦力道偏弱，而
高油價帶來的逆差壓力與生產動能轉弱，將是後續成
長的主要考驗 。基於地緣與油價的不確定性 ，10月
日本央行暫停升息，維持利率不變。
重點摘要
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理

需求，惟能源價格上漲推升進
口成本與貿易逆差。工業生產、
PMI及勞動市場同步降溫，經
濟仍維持「出口強、內需弱」
格局，成長動能面臨考驗。

《日銀暫停升息，12月升息預期支撐利率高檔》
日本央行已於 9月會期將政策利率上調 25個基點至
1.25%，為1995年以來最高 ，也是三個月內第二
次、1990年以來最快的升息節奏。
隨著近期經濟數據 ，10月份日央如預期暫停升息一
碼，會議同步公布《經濟與物價形勢展望報告》，根
據央行經濟預測顯示潛在通膨「已大致達到2%」。
配合植田和男在10月16日記者會曾表示，若基礎通
膨進一步上升將是 「無法接受」的，並在10月19日
國會答詢時表示潛在通膨非常接近2%目標。
加以隨著國際油價再度上破110美元大關，短期政策
重點從「對抗通縮 」轉為「防止通膨超標 」，是以
12月再度升息一碼的機率已大幅提高。
另一方，高市內閣的食品消費稅減稅與國防支出增

加，使市場對財源安排存疑 。不過，10月19日高市
在國會承諾在下一財年預算中妥善管控國債發行規
模，使近期長端利率有所回落 。但12月下一財年預
算與國債發行計畫公布時，財政紀律的可信度將再受
檢驗，預期短線利率上行趨緩，維持高檔盤整。
重點摘要
資料來源：Bloomberg，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理

維持高檔震盪。

資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
《盤勢觀點-資金輪動加速，防禦成長族群接棒》
10月日本股市震盪 、類股表現分化 。市場資金明顯
由上半年漲幅龐大的AI硬體與半導體族群撤出，轉向
軟體服務、醫療保健及內需消費等防禦成長型產業。
其中，東京威力科創、SCREEN、瑞薩電子等指標股
普遍出現雙位數跌幅。相較之下，軟體服務類股大漲
17.9%，BayCurrent、SoftBank等個股表現突出。醫
藥生技、消費零售及娛樂觀光相關族群亦受惠於防禦
需求與內需消費韌性而表現相對亦強勢。值得注意的
是，銀行與非銀行金融股同步走弱，多家大型金融機
構跌幅介於8%至15%，顯示市場擔憂金融條件收緊
可能壓抑經濟活動與資本市場表現。
整體而言，10月日股市場風格由 「AI硬體主導」轉

向「防禦成長與內需消費主導」，同時對經濟基本面
疑慮小幅升溫 ，8~9月相對強勢的金融出現回調壓
力。展望未來，短期盤勢仍受利匯率壓力，以及科技
股評價調整影響 ，加以1H27預估日經225指數企業
獲利年增率轉為負成長，因此本行維持9月以來下調
日股至中性的看法不變，待國際油價回落以及年底年
度預算案底定後，可伺機布局。
重點摘要
5

務、醫療保健及內需消費等防
禦成長族群。半導體、金融與
工業設備股表現疲弱，反映投
資人開始關注高利率環境及企
業獲利成長放緩風險。

《盤勢觀點-澳洲景氣放緩，股匯市轉向盤整》
隨著國際油價維持衝高，澳洲央行（RBA）於9月再
度升息將現金利率提高至 4.6%，官員雖表示若通膨
壓力持續，不排除進一步緊縮。但在高利率與高通膨
壓力持續積累下對景氣負面壓力逐漸加大。
資料顯示，澳洲9月失業率升至4.72%，已連續兩個
月 落 在 澳 央 主 席 認 為 可 有 效 壓 抑 通 膨 的 失 業 率
4.5~5.0%區間；而通膨方面 ，9月份澳洲 CPI雖由
4.0%升至4.3%，但核心CPI僅有2.2%，加以住房通
膨明顯回落，因此本行預期澳央轉向觀望、本輪升息
暫時告終，2027年政策轉向觀望。
外需方面，中國經濟基本面持續低迷，消費與投資不
振亦難以挹注澳洲出口動能，外需對澳幣支撐力量有

限，加以預期聯準會在 12月再度升息 ，將呈現「利
差有撐但優勢遞減」格局，因此預估澳幣短線維持在
0.7~0.71區間盤整。
綜上，持續升息後澳洲基本面轉弱，但高油價壓力又
迫使央行貨幣政策難以轉向寬鬆，加以企業獲利未見
改善動能，澳洲股市難以樂觀，本行維持中性展望。
重點摘要
• 高油價迫使澳洲央行維持偏
鷹態度 ，但高利率已逐步壓
抑就業與內需。
• 中國需求偏弱限制出口前景 ，
加上美澳利差優勢收斂 ，預
期澳幣短期維持區間震盪 ，
澳股展望偏中性。

## Document 486: 【趨勢主題】歐洲市場問答：高利環境下的壓力與機會

Source PDF: all report pdf/【趨勢主題】歐洲市場問答：高利環境下的壓力與機會.pdf
Report source: CTBC
Date: 2026-10-02
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

時性和正確性不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之
需求及投資之風險，並就投資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不
涉及特定投資標的之建議。
歐洲市場問答：高利環境下的壓力與機會
1
Thematic Report
重點提示
中東地緣衝突自2026年2月底爆發以來仍陷僵局，能源價格維
持高檔的時間較預期更長，令歐元區通膨壓力與升息預期升溫。
另一方面，歐洲政府擴大財政支出，公債供給增加，加上歐洲
央行持續縮表，推升期限溢價，令歐洲公債殖利率大幅走升，
德國十年期公債殖利率9/28升破3.6%，創2009年6月以來新高。
本報告透過問答，分析歐洲公債殖利率走高的原因、主權債務
與財政風險、政府因應措施、高利率對企業的影響，以及高利
率環境下的歐洲股債展望。
◼ 本波歐洲公債殖利率走高的驅動因素：能源價格上漲推升通
膨、市場重估歐央貨幣政策路徑，同時，財政支出令歐洲公

債供給增加與歐央持續縮表使私人部門需要吸收更多公債供
給，推升期限溢價，財政疑慮使部分歐洲國家利差承受額外
壓力，加上全球利率上升的外溢影響，共同推升殖利率走高。
◼ 歐洲政府調整發債結構，央行備有穩市工具：歐洲政府縮短
發債期限以控制融資成本，預估此趨勢將延續至2027年；
萬一主權債市場出現無序波動，歐洲央行有OMT與TPI政策
工具，但不會輕易動用。
◼ 整體歐洲債務風險尚可控但中長期壓力持續累積：目前利率
水準尚未對整體債務可持續性構成普遍威脅，但財政脆弱性
較高的國家壓力升高，法國尤其需要關注。財政與政治變數
使法國資產風險溢價維持高位，目前股債定價已反映相當程
度的負面風險。
◼ 德國公債殖利率短期將維持高檔：歐洲央行12月仍有再升息
可能，待能源價格與通膨壓力緩和、升息預期降溫後，利率
才有回落空間，但難降至3%以下。
◼ 歐洲企業資產負債表健康、獲利具韌性，緩衝高利率衝擊。
◼ 高利率限制歐股估值擴張，歐股維持中立，聚焦獲利成長與
政策支撐的產業：銀行受惠於目前利率環境與信貸需求，工

業、電氣化/能源安全及AI受惠戰略自主與資本支出提升。

德國公債殖利率曲線上移，短率升幅大於長率
中東衝突發生以來德國公債殖利率曲線(中東地緣衝突前 vs. 目前)
近期全球公債殖利率大幅上揚 ，歐洲國家亦然，德國10年期公
債殖利率9/28升破3.6%，創2009年6月來新高，若從2月底中

東地緣衝突爆發以來起算 ，德國十年債利率累積彈升了近 100
個基點，30年期德債利率亦逼近4%(至9/28止)。
Q1：什麼因素推動今年歐洲公債殖利率走高？
(1)能源價格上漲推升通膨、市場重估歐央貨幣政策路徑，為驅
動本波利率上揚的主因：
2月底中東衝突爆發以來 ，德國10年期公債殖利率與油氣價格
走勢高度相關。此外，德國公債各期限殖利率全面上移 ，其中
短率升幅高於長率 ，反映本波利率上揚主 要受到能源價格上升
所帶動的通膨風險，以及市場對ECB政策路徑重新定價的影響。
此外，美國殖利率上升透過全球資本市場產生外溢效果 ，帶動
全球無風險利率中樞上移 ，亦對歐洲公債殖利率形成上行壓力 。

歐洲政府發債規模增與ECB縮表同步推升債券供給
■淨債券發行 ■ECB縮表 ◆對私人部門的淨供給
(2)歐洲公債供給增加、央行持續縮表，推升期限溢價：
德國擴大財政支出，歐洲政府融資需求及公債供給增加；同時 ，
ECB持續進行資產負債表正常化 ，逐步減少公債持有量 ，使新
增債券供給需由私人投資人吸收 ，推升投資人要求的期限溢價 。
除此之外，政府債務持有者結構轉變 ，個人與海外投資人等對
價格較為敏感的投資人占比增加，使債市更容易受到市場情緒
與金融條件變化的影響。
(3)財政疑慮使部分歐洲國家利差承受額外壓力：
歐洲部分國家面臨較高的政府債務壓力，加上政治局勢限制財
政調整空間，市場對債務可持續性的疑慮難消 。尤其在年度預
算協商期間，財政脆弱性較高的國家如法國 、英國，主權債務

風險重新定價容易反映在長端殖利率及與德債的利差擴大。

需求及投資之風險，並就投資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不
涉及特定投資標的之建議。
Q2：面對公債殖利率上升，歐洲政府如何因應？
面對公債殖利率上升 ，歐洲政府調整發債結構 、增加短年期債
券發行，以控制高利率環境下的融資成本 ，預期此趨勢將延續
至2027年；惟縮短債務期限雖可降低當期融資成本，也將提高
中期再融資風險。
Q3：萬一歐洲主權債市場出現無序波動，歐洲有哪些
政策工具可以運用？
歐洲央行主要政策工具有二 ，分別為直接貨幣交易 (OMT)與傳
導保護工具(TPI)。
(1)直接貨幣交易(OMT)：2012年9月推出，源自歐債危機，可
在次級市場購買符合條件國家的1至3年期政府公債，且購買
規模事前不設上限 ，惟須搭配歐洲穩定機制 (ESM)的金融援
助計畫，並具嚴格的附帶條件，使用門檻較高，由主權國家

主動申請。
(2)傳導保護工具 (TPI)：2022年7月推出 ，可在次級市場購買
符合條件國家的 1至10年期政府部門債券 ，目的在於因應與
該國基本面不相稱的無序市場波動 ，避免金融市場分裂影響
貨幣政策傳導。啟動時將評估該國是否符合歐盟財政規範 、
債務是否具備可持續性等條件 ，由歐央評估後決定是否啟動。
不過，OMT與TPI均屬市場失序時的最後手段 ，並非用於壓低
正常的主權債殖利率或消除國家間的基本面利差 ，啟動有嚴格
條件，歐央不會輕易動用，目前此兩項工具均未實際動用過。
高利率下，歐洲政府調整發債、央行備有穩市工具

涉及特定投資標的之建議。
Q4：歐洲政府債務的可持續性是否有立即風險？
主要歐洲國家除德國與西班牙外 ，IMF預估2026年政府債務佔
GDP比重均超過 100%。同時，近年地緣衝突頻率升高 ，戰略
自主與強化地區安全已成為歐洲核心目標 。為了讓成員國有擴
大國防支出的空間 ，歐盟允許成員國啟動 國家豁免條款，得以
在增加國防支出的同時 ，暫時不受歐盟財政規則約束 ，截至目
前已有18個歐盟會員國申請。國防支出提升，若未搭配增加財
政收入或其他支出調整，將使政府債務維持在較高路徑。
所幸，從利率與經濟成長率差距來看 ，目前利率水準尚未對歐
洲整體債務可持續性構成 立即威脅，但部分高負債國家的債務
壓力仍須留意。過去十年低利率環境的效益逐步消退 ，利率與

經濟成長率的差距 (r－g)較過去不利 ，加上期限溢價回升 ，使
高負債國家的債務動態面臨風險。
檢視歐元區四大經濟體 ，德國、西班牙政府債務的利率低於經
濟成長率，債務動態相對穩健；義大利政府債務 佔GDP比重最
高，所幸在政府控制支出下，自2024年以來維持初級財政盈餘，
有助控制債務比率進一步擴大。法國利率雖略低於經濟成長率 ，
但政府債務已接近 GDP的120%，在財政赤字偏高 、利息支出
持續增加且財政整頓進展有限 的情況下，未來兩年債務佔GDP
比率預期將持續上升，財政脆弱性相對較高。
整體歐洲債務風險可控但中長期壓力正在累積

Q5：近期法德利差顯著擴大，驅動因素與對法國股債
市的影響？
財政與政治不確定性升溫 ，推升法國主權風險溢價 ：法國步入
財政與政治不確定性升溫時期 ，不利的財政前景 ，加上4Q26
預算協商與2027年將舉行總統大選，政治不確定性升溫推升十
年期法德利差9月下旬突破100個基點，9/28升至112個基點、
創2012年6月以來新高。法國總統大選將於2027年4~5月舉行，
4/18舉行第一輪投票 ，若無候選人過半 ，5/2將舉行第二輪投
票。由於法國政治版圖高度分裂 、且極右派支持度領先 ，相較
於過往選舉周期，2027年法國選舉的不確定更高，預估將令市
場在2027年總統大選前維持謹慎情緒。
民調反映政治版圖分裂 ，增加財政政策的不確定性： 民調顯示，
極右派國民聯盟候選人瑪琳·勒龐在總統大選首輪的支持度領先，
超過三成，極左派法國不屈黨候選人梅朗雄 、中間偏右候選人
愛德華·菲利普民調落在15~18%左右。法國大選高機率將會進
入第二輪投票，取決於進入第二輪的候選人 ，目前民調顯示以

中間偏右候選人菲利普最有希望擊敗勒龐 ，若為極左派梅朗雄
進入第二輪，則勒龐勝選機率較高。

極端政治勢力候選人在本次大選勝選的可能性提高 ，為本已脆
弱的法國財政增添另一層不確定性 。與以往政治週期不同 ，法
國進入2027年大選週期時，財政迴旋餘地相當有限，加上法國
經濟成長弱於預期與利息成本上升，加大財政調整難度。
股債市影響：財政與政治變數將令法國資產風險溢價維持高位
(1)債市： 巴克萊資本 (2026/8)預估三種情境： (a)正向情境：
中東衝突告一段落 、秋季預算協商順利 、中間派候選人民調上
升，十年期法德利差有機會收斂至60~65個基點；(b)不利情境：
秋季預算協商引發市場雜音 、中東衝突進一步推高升息預期 ，
總統大選民調維持較高不確定性 ，法 德 利差 預 估 將 擴 大 至

95~100個基點；(c)極端情境：沒有中間派候選人進入第二輪
總統大選，法德利差可能突破100個基點。
9/28十年期法德利差已升至 112個基點，顯示市場已反映相當
程度的政治風險，再大幅惡化的空間應有限 。儘管如此，不確
定性短期難消，預估法德利差仍將維持在(b)情境高檔盤整。
(2)股市：法國股市估值相較歐股處於折價，且投資人對法國股
市的配置已明顯降低 ，有助緩和股市大幅修正的風險 。然而，
短線亦缺乏驅動股市行情的催化劑 ，法國經濟成長預測持續下
修、經濟動能落後歐元區 ，加上政治不確定性將影響投資人配
置意願，預估法國股市在2027年4月總統大選前波動仍大 ，金
融與內需導向型企業估值預估將承壓。
